Analyzing the classifier head reveals that orientation information from the representations' structure is actively used, improving performance by 4.90% across all datasets. Our results highlight the sample-efficiency benefits of equivariance and the value of retained orientation information, motivating further investigation beyond image classification. Code is available at https://github.com/hlzl/equivariant-convnext.

</details>


### [140] [Spe3DUp: Faithful Trajectory Distillation for Fast Image-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34141)
*Liangchao Xu,Jiaye Fu,Youtian Lin,Feihu Zhang,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spe3DUp 提出基于忠实轨迹蒸馏（FTD）的粗到细框架，在保持参考图像对应性的同时大幅加速 3D 网格生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该文面向图像参考驱动的 3D 网格生成任务（如游戏资产重建、产品数字化），输入为参考图像，输出为需与输入物体高度对应的复杂结构网格，目标是在保证保真度的前提下实现快速推理。

Motivation: 现有高质量 3D 生成依赖长多步采样与大规模网络反复前向，推理耗时长；而参考驱动任务要求生成几何忠实重建特定参考物体，因此加速不能以牺牲对应性为代价，需要压缩采样计算同时保留重建能力。

Method: 提出 Spe3DUp 粗到细框架，核心为忠实轨迹蒸馏（FTD）：用教师模型以输入为条件的去噪轨迹直接监督少步学生模型，以保留其生成行为；FTD 分别作用于粗几何生成与几何精修两阶段，压缩二者采样过程并维持与参考输入的对应；此外通过高效几何解码与并行重网格化加速完整网格生成流程。

实验效果: 实验表明 Spe3DUp 的几何质量与输入对应性接近教师模型，粗生成与精修 DiT 分别加速 10.0× 和 19.9×，端到端相较原始教师流水线实现 7.7× 加速。

Abstract: Recent 3D generative models can produce high-quality meshes with complex structures and fine geometric details, with broad applications in games, film, and digital design. However, high-quality generation typically relies on long multi-step sampling processes that repeatedly evaluate large networks, leading to long inference times. At the same time, reference-driven tasks such as game-asset reconstruction and product digitization demand close correspondence to the input image: generated geometry must faithfully reconstruct the specific reference object. Acceleration must therefore reduce sampling computation while preserving the model's ability to reconstruct that object. To enable fast and faithful 3D generation, we introduce Spe3DUp, a coarse-to-fine framework based on Faithful Trajectory Distillation (FTD). FTD directly supervises few-step students with the teacher's input-conditioned denoising trajectories to preserve its generation behavior. We apply FTD separately to coarse geometry generation and geometric refinement, compressing both sampling processes while maintaining correspondence to the reference input. We further accelerate the complete mesh-generation pipeline with efficient geometry decoding and parallel remeshing. Experiments show that Spe3DUp maintains geometric quality and input correspondence close to those of the teacher, accelerates the coarse and refinement DiTs by 10.0x and 19.9x, respectively, and achieves a 7.7x end-to-end speedup over the original teacher pipeline.

</details>


### [141] [Non-Linear Motion from Two Frames: Cubic Hermite Trajectories for Video Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34143)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Abtahi Majeed Chowdhury,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Jaesik Choi,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HermiteVFI用三次Hermite曲线为每个像素建模一条显式连续运动轨迹，替代对端点光流的查询时刻重采样，从而更好处理非匀速运动与高倍率插帧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 双帧视频插帧的任务是由一对端点帧及其查询时刻t生成中间帧，目标是在任意t处合成与真实运动一致的高质量中间画面。

Motivation: 现有方法按查询时刻对端点光流做重采样，默认采用匀速直线轨迹，但相同的端点位移其实对应多种中间轨迹，导致难以刻画非匀速、弯曲的真实运动，尤其在高插帧倍率下误差显著。

Method: 方法为每像素定义一条连续轨迹：端点位移固定曲线的两个位置边界，两个隐式端点导数（由帧对学习得到，并借助外观导出的方向线索辅助推断）控制内部路径；三次Hermite曲线是能独立满足两位置两导数共四个边界量的最低次多项式。其残差形式可精确包含线性运动、对任意网络输出都结构性地保持端点位移，系数每帧对只预测一次并以闭式在任意t求值。作者进一步指出单个内部时刻对两自由系数仅构成秩亏约束，而任意两个不同内部时刻在表示层面满秩，故采用多时刻监督。

实验效果: 在X4K1000FPS、SNU-FILM与Vimeo90K-Septuplet上，最大增益出现在困难的非匀速运动与高插帧倍率场景；摘要未给出具体数值。理论证据为上述秩亏/满秩分析及残差形式的结构性性质；轨迹系数预测可摊还，而反向、细化与合成仍依赖查询时刻。

Abstract: Two-frame video frame interpolation commonly resamples endpoint optical flow by the query time, which selects a constant-velocity straight-line path even though the same endpoint displacement is compatible with many intermediate trajectories. We present HermiteVFI, which replaces independent query-time motion prediction with one explicit continuous trajectory per pixel. The measured endpoint displacement fixes the two trajectory positions, while two latent endpoint derivatives learned from the frame pair control the interior path. A cubic Hermite curve is the minimum-degree polynomial that can independently satisfy these two position and two derivative boundary quantities. Its residual form contains linear motion exactly, structurally preserves the measured displacement endpoints for any network output, and has coefficients that are predicted once per frame pair and evaluated in closed form at arbitrary t. We further show that one interior query time gives a rank-deficient constraint on the two free trajectory coefficients, whereas any two distinct interior times are full rank at the representation level, motivating multi-time supervision. Appearance-derived directional cues help infer the latent derivatives that endpoint displacement alone cannot identify. On X4K1000FPS, SNU-FILM, and Vimeo90K-Septuplet, the largest gains occur for difficult non-uniform motion and high interpolation ratios. The amortization applies to trajectory-coefficient prediction; downstream reversal, refinement, and synthesis remain query dependent.

</details>


### [142] [Lost in the Grid: Where Multimodal LLMs Fail Between Seeing and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34152)
*Luoxi Zhang,Tianyi Tan,Jiawei Liu,Xingyu Zeng,Min Yang,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GridProbe是一个诊断式基准，用于定位多模态大模型在空间规划任务中失败于视觉感知还是规划阶段，并通过针对性微调验证诊断结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该研究关注多模态大模型的空间规划能力：模型需解读视觉布局并输出到达目标的动作序列。网格游戏（如Sokoban）因规则明确、每步可验证而成为常用测试平台，输入为棋盘布局（图像或等价文本网格），输出为动作序列，目标是准确衡量并改进模型的规划表现。

Motivation: 现有基准多只报告端到端成功率，无法区分失败究竟源于视觉感知、规则应用还是多步规划，因而难以指明改进方向。

Method: 在Sokoban上将端到端求解与四项探针（识别、定位、单步预测、状态追踪）结合，并为每个棋盘同时提供图像与等价文本网格，从而定位失败阶段；随后通过针对性微调验证诊断：仅对受限于感知的模型微调视觉模块，并检验其对规则受限模型的无效性及在未见迷宫游戏上的零样本迁移。

实验效果: 在Sokoban与迷宫游戏上评测七个MLLM，发现成功率相近背后瓶颈不同：专有模型受限于多步规划，开源模型受限于规则应用或视觉网格索引（行列计数）；规划变易时同一模型的视觉代价从7.6升至37.7分。仅微调感知受限模型的视觉模块使交互式Sokoban成功率由5.8%升至20.4%（约3.5倍），并零样本迁移至迷宫（单轮成功率13.8%至67.1%），而同样数据对规则受限模型无效，用求解器解训练亦无增益。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) excel at static visual understanding and are increasingly applied to spatial planning, where a model interprets a visual layout and outputs an action sequence to reach a goal. Grid games are a common testbed for this ability because their rules are explicit and a solver can verify every move. Yet existing benchmarks mostly report end-to-end success rates, which hide whether a failure stems from visual perception, rule application, or multi-step planning. We introduce GridProbe, a diagnostic benchmark that 1) locates the failing stage between visual perception and planning and 2) verifies the diagnosis by targeted fine-tuning. On Sokoban, GridProbe pairs end-to-end solving with four probes (recognition, localization, single-step prediction, and state tracking) and gives every board both as an image and as an equivalent text grid. Evaluating seven MLLMs on Sokoban and a maze game reveals distinct bottlenecks behind similar success rates: proprietary models are limited by multi-step planning, whereas open-source models are limited by rule application or by visual grid indexing, i.e., counting rows and columns. Planning failures also hide perception failures: when planning is easier, the visual cost of the same model grows from 7.6 to 37.7 points. The diagnosis identifies what to train: fine-tuning only the vision module of the perception-limited model on board reading raises its interactive Sokoban success 3.5-fold (5.8% to 20.4%) and transfers zero-shot to the unseen maze game (single-turn success 13.8% to 67.1%), whereas the same data does not help a rule-limited model and training on solver solutions brings no gain. We will release the benchmark and code at https://github.com/suat-llm/GridProbe.

</details>


### [143] [HSTTA: Hierarchical Selective Test-Time Adaptation for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34153)
*Hongzhou Chen,Yuhao Wei,Lianghua He,Yihang Liu,Zengqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HSTTA 提出一种分层选择性测试时自适应框架，按每个 EEG 查询的预测置信度匹配伪标签监督粒度，显著缓解跨被试 EEG-to-image 检索中的被试漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 跨被试 EEG-to-image 检索需用预训练模型将目标被试的无标签 EEG 查询映射到图库图像并排序检索。由于被试漂移，模型在新被试上性能大幅下降，目标是在无标签条件下提升目标被试的检索准确率。

Motivation: 现有 TTA 方法难以让监督粒度与每个查询的预测置信度相匹配，导致大量查询无法获得可靠伪标签，自适应效果受限。

Method: 在图库图像嵌入上构建层次结构，用分层选择性预测为每个 EEG 查询分配与其当前置信度相符的监督粒度并据此构造自适应目标；提出跨会话一致性校准（CSCC），以无监督方式将全局置信度阈值校准至预设准确率水平；并提出互惠分层回退（RHB），将缺乏反向匹配支持的查询移至更粗节点以抑制错误强化。

实验效果: 在两个公开基准及消融实验中验证了有效性；在 THINGS-EEG2 上，cosine（CSLS）排序下 Top-1/Top-5 分别比最强 EEG 专用 TTA 基线高出 17.9（13.4）与 8.9（3.7）个百分点，且自适应过程中的伪标签更准确。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG-to-image retrieval suffers from substantial performance degradation on new subjects due to subject shift. Test-time adaptation (TTA) can mitigate this shift by adapting pretrained models with unlabeled target-subject EEG. However, most existing TTA methods struggle to match supervision granularity to each query's prediction confidence, leaving many queries without reliable pseudo-labels. To address this, we propose a novel framework named Hierarchical Selective Test-Time Adaptation (HSTTA). HSTTA builds a hierarchy over gallery image embeddings, uses hierarchical selective prediction to assign each EEG query a supervision granularity matching its current prediction confidence, and constructs adaptation objectives accordingly. We further propose Cross-Session Consistency Calibration (CSCC), an unsupervised method that calibrates the global confidence threshold to target a predefined accuracy level for hierarchical predictions. Moreover, we introduce Reciprocal Hierarchical Backoff (RHB), which moves queries lacking reverse-matching support to coarser nodes to mitigate error reinforcement. Experiments on two public benchmarks and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of HSTTA and its key components. On THINGS-EEG2, HSTTA outperforms the strongest EEG-specific TTA baseline by 17.9 (13.4) and 8.9 (3.7) percentage points in Top-1/Top-5 accuracy, respectively, under cosine (CSLS) ranking. Further analyses show that HSTTA yields more accurate pseudo-labels during adaptation, supporting more reliable model updates.

</details>


### [144] [OceanFix: Reference-Guided Diffusion for Sparse-View Underwater 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34155)
*Gen Li,Maoxin Chen,Yao Xiao,Xinyang Li,Yu Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OceanFix 是一个参考引导的稀疏视角水下 3D 重建框架，将扩散式视角修复与高斯场景精修结合，以缓解稀疏观测下的区域欠约束与新视角伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是基于稀疏视角的水下图像进行三维重建并合成新视角。输入为稀疏的水下参考观测（含相机位姿、参考深度），输出为可渲染的高斯三维场景，目标是在光衰减与后向散射条件下提升渲染质量。

Motivation: 现有高斯泼溅水下重建方法在稀疏观测、光衰减与后向散射下仍使部分场景区域欠约束，导致新视角渲染出现明显伪影；同时修复得到的视角难以有效回流到三维场景中。

Method: 采用两阶段训练的扩散修复模型：第一阶段从参考观测与估计的介质特征中学习水下外观；第二阶段利用相机位姿与参考深度对齐参考特征，并以相对深度监督引导结构修复（推理时无需目标视角深度）。在此基础上提出介质感知蒸馏，按几何修复需求与通道级光学可观测性将修复视角迁移到三维场景；通过渲染—修复—蒸馏的迭代循环精修欠约束区域，同时保留观测视角的监督。

实验效果: 摘要仅给出定性的实验结论：在水下数据集上，OceanFix 提升了稀疏视角三维重建的渲染质量，减少了可见伪影并恢复更精细的场景细节；未报告具体基准、对比方法与数值指标。

Abstract: Recent advances in Gaussian splatting have enabled efficient underwater reconstruction, but sparse observations, light attenuation, and backscatter still leave scene regions underconstrained and cause novel-view artifacts. We present OceanFix, a reference-guided framework for sparse-view underwater 3D reconstruction that combines diffusion-based view repair with Gaussian scene refinement. Our repair model follows a two-stage training strategy that separates underwater appearance adaptation from geometric refinement. The first stage learns underwater appearance from reference observations and estimated medium features. The second uses camera poses and reference depth to align reference features, with relative-depth supervision guiding structural repair without requiring target-view depth at inference. We further propose medium-aware distillation that transfers repaired views into the 3D scene according to geometric repair demand and channel-specific optical observability. Through iterative rendering, repair, and distillation, OceanFix refines underconstrained regions while retaining supervision from the observed views. Experiments on underwater datasets demonstrate that OceanFix improves rendering quality in sparse-view 3D reconstruction, reducing visible artifacts and recovering finer scene details.

</details>


### [145] [Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding for Temporally Grounded Audio-Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34162)
*Minyeong Jeong,Yeonju Kim,Yong Man Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过音频-视觉同步探针发现AV-LLM对音视频时间对齐变化不敏感，并提出免训练的解码方法MSCD以强化时间对齐信息的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 论文考察音频-视觉大语言模型（AV-LLM）是否真正利用其所表征的音视频时间结构来关联声音与视觉事件。任务输入为音视频及与其对齐相关的查询，输出为模型对相应视觉事件的预测；目标是使其预测由时间对齐的音视频共同支持。

Motivation: 现有AV-LLM虽被设计为表征音视频时间结构，但其能否有效利用这些时间信息尚不明确；探针实验显示，当音频被移位使查询声音与另一视觉事件重叠时，模型仍偏向原本对齐的事件，说明其对音视频时间对齐变化的敏感度有限。

Method: 提出Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding（MSCD），一种免训练的解码方法：通过对比"时间对齐保持"与"时间对齐被破坏"两种条件下的logits，突出由对齐音视频共同支持的预测。对齐破坏方式为在对另一模态保持不变的前提下，对音频或视频之一进行时间移位。

实验效果: 在两个AV-LLM和三个基准上的实验表明MSCD有效提升时间接地的音视频理解；在Daily-Omni上，OmniVinci与video-SALMONN 2+的准确率分别较基模型提升2.2和2.8个百分点。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (AV-LLMs) are designed to represent the temporal structure of audio and video, yet it remains unclear how effectively they use this temporal information to relate sounds to visual events. To examine this, we probe an AV-LLM on an audio-visual synchronization task by shifting the audio so that the queried sound overlaps a different visual event. We observe that the model still favors the originally aligned event over the newly aligned one, indicating limited sensitivity to changes in audio-visual temporal alignment. To address this, we propose Modality-Shift Contrastive Decoding (MSCD), a training-free decoding method that emphasizes audio-visual temporal alignment in model predictions. MSCD contrasts logits under preserved and disrupted temporal alignment to favor predictions supported by aligned audio and video. We disrupt this alignment by temporally shifting either the audio or the video, while keeping the other modality fixed. Extensive evaluations with two AV-LLMs across three benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of MSCD for temporally grounded audio-visual understanding. In particular, MSCD improves accuracy over the base models on Daily-Omni by 2.2 and 2.8 percentage points for OmniVinci and video-SALMONN 2+, respectively, suggesting that emphasizing temporal alignment can also benefit broader audio-visual understanding.

</details>


### [146] [Admoab-DETR: Resolution-Tiered Fusion for Infrared-Visible Object Detection with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34163)
*Wenchao Li,Haoran Jia,Shuyuan Wen,Keyan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Admoab-DETR，一种以单套权重同时支持红外、可见光及成对输入的实时检测器，通过分辨率分层融合与模式匹配特征蒸馏实现精度与效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 面向全天候目标检测的红外—可见光图像提供互补线索，任务是在传感器偶发失效导致单模态缺失或双模态可用时，用同一模型完成目标检测并输出检测结果。

Motivation: 现有成对输入检测器在传感器掉线时性能下降，且模态自适应方法在所有尺度上统一施加重型跨模态注意力，在高分辨率特征图上产生过高的 token 开销。

Method: 提出分辨率分层融合（RTF），按空间 token 密度与特征粒度分配融合能力：最高分辨率尺度用轻量空间模态门控做精细局部融合并支持零成本单视图直通，较粗语义尺度用通道压缩的可变形 Admoab neck 建模跨模态对应；同时提出模式匹配特征蒸馏（MMFD），以与可用模态同步的全融合教师对齐多尺度编码器特征，避免病态跨模态幻觉。

实验效果: 实验表明 Admoab-DETR 在缺失模态与成对基准上取得最优的精度—效率权衡；相较基线参数量减少 18.1%、成对推理延迟降低 39.6%，且无额外推理开销。作者另构建了严格配准的 20,000 对红外—可见光基准 Admoab。

Abstract: Infrared and visible images offer complementary cues for all-weather object detection, yet real-world sensor dropouts can leave one stream unavailable and degrade paired-input detectors. Meanwhile, existing modality-adaptive methods apply heavy cross-modal attention uniformly across scales, incurring prohibitive token overhead on high-resolution maps. We propose Admoab-DETR, a unified real-time detector supporting infrared-only, visible-only, and paired inputs with a single set of weights. Specifically, \emph{Resolution-Tiered Fusion} (RTF) allocates fusion capacity by spatial token density and feature granularity: a lightweight Spatial Modality Gate governs fine local fusion at the highest-resolution scale with zero-cost single-view pass-through, while channel-compressed deformable Admoab necks capture cross-modal correspondence at coarser semantic scales. Furthermore, \emph{Mode-Matched Feature Distillation} (MMFD) aligns multi-scale encoder features with an availability-synchronized full-fusion teacher, avoiding ill-posed cross-modal hallucination while cutting parameters by 18.1\% and paired latency by 39.6\% at zero inference overhead. We also construct Admoab, a strictly registered 20,000-pair infrared--visible benchmark across diverse scenes. Experiments show Admoab-DETR achieves state-of-the-art accuracy--efficiency trade-offs across missing-modality and paired benchmarks. Our code will be available at https://github.com/liwenchao0615/Admoab-DETR.

</details>


### [147] [Loop4D: Embodied World Modeling with 4D VAEs and Looped Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34199)
*Gen Li,Fan Luo,Mengjun Lin,Shu Han,Bohan Li,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Loop4D 通过保持运动的 4D 表征与循环生成式精炼，实现机器人操作场景中 RGB 与几何（点图）的联合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务旨在预测机器人操作的结果，需同时建模视觉外观与演化的 3D 几何，即输入 RGB 与点图序列并输出未来 4D（RGB+几何）视频预测。

Motivation: 现有方法存在两点局限：视频压缩会模糊细粒度机器人运动细节；联合预测 RGB 与几何需要足够的生成容量，而单纯堆叠参数代价高。

Method: 提出 4D VAE 将 RGB 与点图序列编码为紧凑时空潜变量：RGB VAE 结合几何引导监督与预测式重建以抑制压缩引起的运动伪影，几何 VAE 用轻量适配器复用预训练 RGB 表征先验；其上构建双流循环扩散 Transformer，外观与几何分流但共享 Transformer 权重并设跨模态连接，循环复用 Transformer 块以在不复制主干参数的情况下提升有效深度、以更多计算换取更强联合预测。

实验效果: 摘要仅报告定性结论：实验实现高质量 4D 生成，并在仿真机器人操作任务中验证了 Loop4D 的有效性，未给出具体基准、对比方法或数值指标。

Abstract: Predicting the outcomes of robotic manipulation requires modeling both visual appearance and evolving 3D geometry. This poses two challenges: video compression can obscure fine-grained robot motion, while jointly predicting RGB and geometry demands sufficient generative capacity. We introduce Loop4D, a framework that addresses these challenges through motion-preserving 4D representations and recurrent generative refinement. Our 4D VAE encodes RGB and pointmap sequences into compact spatiotemporal latents while preserving motion details and geometric structure. Our RGB VAE combines geometry-guided supervision with predictive reconstruction to reduce compression-induced motion artifacts. Our geometry VAE uses lightweight adapters to adapt pretrained RGB representations while preserving their priors. Building on these representations, our Dual-Stream Looped Diffusion Transformer maintains separate appearance and geometry streams with shared Transformer weights and cross-modal connections. By reusing Transformer blocks across loops, we increase effective depth without replicating backbone parameters, enabling models with fewer parameters to improve joint RGB-geometry prediction through additional computation. Experiments demonstrate high-quality 4D generation and validate Loop4D in simulated robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Logits: Coherent Hallucination Mitigation via Attention Contrastive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34207)
*Yujia Chen,Rui Sun,Huayu Mai,Wangkai Li,Zhangyu He,Bingzhou Wang,Aibing Li,Wenzhang Sun,Tianzhu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Attention Contrastive Decoding (ACD)，一种免训练插件，将部分对比操作前移至注意力机制层，与现有 logit 级对比解码方法互补，在减少幻觉的同时提升生成连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务目标是缓解大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）的多模态幻觉，即生成与事实不符的内容。已有 Contrastive Decoding (CD) 通过对比「业余」与「专家」分支的 logit 输出来抑制幻觉，目标是实现可信的多模态生成。

Motivation: 作者发现 logit 级干预会从根本上损害生成连贯性，因而不得不引入与幻觉抑制无关的、限制性的惩罚约束；这一内在矛盾说明仅在前向末端做对比不足以兼顾连贯性与幻觉抑制。

Method: ACD 免训练、即插即用，将部分对比操作从 logit 层迁移至注意力机制，在更早的前向阶段施加干预；通过自适应减法策略（ASS）进行平滑、语义保持的干预，削弱与幻觉相关的注意力模式，同时增强关键的视觉信息，从而与 logit 级 CD 形成互补。

实验效果: 摘要未报告具体基准、对比数值或定量指标；仅定性指出将 ACD 与现有 CD 方法结合（如 VCD+ACD）可产生明显更连贯的输出，进一步减少幻觉，并无需限制性惩罚约束。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate impressive multimodal capabilities, yet suffer from hallucination - generating factually inaccurate content. Contrastive Decoding (CD) mitigates this by contrasting amateur and expert branches at the logit level. However, our investigation reveals that such logit-level interventions fundamentally compromise generation coherence, necessitating restrictive penalty constraints unrelated to hallucination suppression.
  We introduce Attention Contrastive Decoding (ACD), a training-free plug-in that complements logit-level CD by relocating part of the contrastive operations to the attention mechanism. Operating at an earlier stage of the forward pass, ACD performs smooth semantic-preserving interventions through an Adaptive Subtraction Strategy (ASS), which attenuates hallucination-associated attention patterns while amplifying critical visual information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that combining ACD with existing CD methods (e.g., VCD+ACD) produces substantially more coherent outputs with further reduced hallucinations, eliminating restrictive penalties while enabling trustworthy multimodal generation.

</details>


### [149] [GeoCAM: Self-Supervised Sonar-Optical Representation Learning via Geographic Overlap Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34218)
*Fadi Abdeladhim Zidi,Andrea De Salve,Giuseppe Celeste,Abdellah Zakaria Sellam,Paolo Spagnolo,Cosimo Distante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoCAM 是一个地理引导的跨模态自监督框架，在无语义标注条件下为配对侧扫声呐与水下光学影像学习共享嵌入空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该研究面向海底生境理解中的跨模态表征学习任务：输入为侧扫声呐条块及对应的多幅光学视角图像（含GPS等地理元数据），输出为可用于跨模态检索与对齐的共享嵌入空间，目标是在大量无标注海洋数据上获得语义有意义且几何一致的表示。

Motivation: 现有方法未能充分利用真实光声联合调查中普遍存在的"一对多"对应关系以及地理元数据，导致跨模态自监督在该场景下研究不足，难以从无标注海洋数据中学到语义与几何兼具的表示。

Method: GeoCAM 由三部分组成：(i) Set Attention Aggregator，自适应地将多个光学观测汇聚为单一条块级表示，以处理一对多对应；(ii) 几何加权 InfoNCE 目标，将GPS推导的足迹重叠度转化为软正样本监督，注入几何一致性；(iii) 可选的跨模态掩码重建分支，促进具有物理依据的跨模态对齐。

实验效果: 在 BenthiCat SSS-CAM 数据集上的实验表明，GeoCAM 能有效学习判断哪些声呐与相机样本描述同一海底斑块，并在跨模态检索与对齐上优于强对比学习基线；摘要未报告具体数值指标。

Abstract: Cross-modal self-supervised learning for paired side-scan sonar and underwater optical imagery is a promising direction for benthic habitat understanding, but it remains underexplored because existing methods do not fully exploit the one-to-many correspondences and geographic metadata available in real opti-acoustic surveys. This limits the ability to learn representations that are both semantically meaningful and geometrically consistent from abundant unlabeled marine data. To address this problem, we present GeoCAM, a geography-guided framework that learns a shared embedding space for sonar tiles and their associated optical views without semantic annotations. GeoCAM achieves its goal by combining three components: (i) a Set Attention Aggregator that adaptively summarizes multiple optical observations into a single tile-level representation; (ii) a geometry-weighted InfoNCE objective that converts GPS-derived footprint overlap into soft positive supervision; and (iii) an optional cross-modal masked reconstruction branch that encourages physically grounded cross-modal alignment. Experiments on the BenthiCat SSS-CAM dataset show that GeoCAM can be effectively exploited to learn which sonar and camera samples describe the same seabed patch, improving cross-modal retrieval and alignment over strong contrastive baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/FadiZidiDz/GeoCAM.

</details>


### [150] [Variation-aware Temporal Correspondence for Self-Supervised Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34220)
*Yang Liu,Qianqian Xu,Peisong Wen,Siran Dai,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 VT-CoRe 框架，通过变化感知的时间对应关系提升掩码视频建模的表征学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 自监督视频表征学习利用大规模无标签视频，通过掩码视频建模（MVM）在恢复被掩码内容的过程中从时间上下文中提取语义信息，从而学习视频表征。

Motivation: 视频中的视觉对象在跨帧过程中位置、外观与运动状态持续变化，现有 MVM 方法需在捕捉对应区域时间变化的同时保持语义一致性，但缺乏可靠的时间对应建立手段，也缺少兼顾语义一致性与时间变化的合适表征学习目标。

Method: 针对挑战一，设计三明治采样策略构建双侧时间参考，并提出 Patch Registration Module（PRM）建立可靠的块级对应；针对挑战二，借助自蒸馏架构对配准后的表征施加隐空间语义对齐，并提出时间变化建模以约束表征随时间的演化轨迹。

实验效果: 在 16 个基准、8 项下游任务上验证了有效性，覆盖像素级、帧级、视频级及视觉机器人学习场景；表征分析显示 VT-CoRe 学到了更结构化的隐空间，但摘要未报告具体数值指标。

Abstract: Self-supervised video representation learning has achieved remarkable progress by leveraging large-scale unlabeled video data. Recent methods commonly extend Masked Image Modeling (MIM) to Masked Video Modeling (MVM), where video representations are learned by capturing semantic information from temporal contexts during masked content recovery. Unlike static images, videos exhibit inherent temporal variation, as visual objects continuously change their positions, appearances, and motion states across frames. Therefore, MVM needs to preserve semantic consistency while capturing temporal changes of corresponding regions, which raises two central challenges: 1) how to establish reliable temporal correspondence across frames, and 2) how to formulate an appropriate representation learning objective for modeling both semantic consistency and temporal variation. To address these challenges, we propose Variation-aware Temporal Correspondence for video Representation learning (VT-CoRe) framework. For challenge 1), VT-CoRe introduces a sandwich sampling strategy to construct two-sided temporal references and a Patch Registration Module (PRM) to establish reliable patch-level correspondence. For challenge 2), VT-CoRe imposes latent-space semantic alignment on registered representations through a self-distillation architecture and further proposes temporal variation modeling to constrain representation trajectories over time. Experiments on 8 downstream tasks across 16 benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and applicability of VT-CoRe across pixel-level, frame-level, video-level, and vision-based robotic learning scenarios. Complementary representation analysis shows that VT-CoRe learns a more structured latent space for variation-aware temporal correspondence learning.

</details>


### [151] [AnyCam4D: Ray-Based 4D Gaussian Rendering for Arbitrary Camera Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34223)
*Sascha Kirch,Rafael Pagés,Jan Ondřej,Clara Pérez-Molina,Sergio Martin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyCam4D 通过基于光线的光栅化直接在原始（非针孔）footage 上训练原生 4D 高斯，并用数值稳定的 4D 协方差平方根分解获取高斯的朝向与尺度，从而保留视场与分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务是从真实相机（含广角/鱼眼、卷帘快门等非针孔成像）拍摄的视频中重建可渲染的动态 4D 场景表示；输入为原始 footage，输出为可直接按原相机成像模型渲染的高斯模型，目标是保留视场与细节。

Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅普遍假设针孔相机，需先做去畸变，导致广角与鱼眼视频损失视场或细节；静态高斯泼溅已用光线渲染解决该问题，但原生 4D 高斯直接在时空建模运动，无法从中可靠地从协方差恢复每个高斯的朝向与尺度，因而无法直接套用光线渲染。

Method: 提出 AnyCam4D，采用基于光线（ray-based）的光栅化，在原始未去畸变 footage 上训练原生 4D 高斯，对任意相机模型适用，单步开销约为标准投影水平。关键贡献是对 4D 协方差做平方根条件化处理，数值稳定地分解出高斯的朝向与尺度，从而打通光线渲染与 4D 高斯之间的缺口。

实验效果: 在合成 170° 鱼眼相机上，直接以原图训练使 PSNR 提升 3.0–8.1 dB；真实数据上感知误差低于逐相机适配的标准投影，PSNR 相当且无需相机专用推导；合成相机相对采集装置视角旋转 60–80° 时，相对真值领先 6.2 dB。

Abstract: Real cameras are not pinholes, yet most dynamic Gaussian splatting assumes they are. Footage is therefore undistorted first, losing field of view or detail, especially with wide-angle and fisheye cameras. Static Gaussian splatting has largely solved this with ray-based rendering, which suits any camera. Native 4D Gaussians, which model motion in space-time, cannot use it directly: each Gaussian's orientation and size cannot be recovered reliably from its covariance. We present AnyCam4D, which trains native 4D Gaussians on original footage by ray-based rasterization, preserving field of view and resolution at about the per-step cost of standard projection. Our key contribution is a numerically stable way to obtain orientation and size: we condition a square root of the 4D covariance. Trained models render through other cameras, with rolling shutter, depth of field or chromatic aberration. With a synthetic 170-degree fisheye camera, training on original images improves peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) by 3.0-8.1 dB. On real footage, AnyCam4D has lower perceptual error than the standard projection adapted to each camera, at comparable PSNR and without camera-specific derivations. With the synthetic camera rotated 60-80 degrees away from the capture rig's view, it leads by 6.2 dB against ground truth.

</details>


### [152] [EgoMyo: Physical Hand State Estimation from Egocentric Video and Wrist Electromyography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34224)
*Guanglong Sun,Kanglei Zhou,Ziteng Wang,Nana Wang,Hengkai Tan,Xiaolong Liu,Hang Su,Yi Zhong,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出从第一视角视频与腕部sEMG联合估计物理手部状态的任务，构建EgoMyo基准并提出MyoBridge融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该论文研究从第一视角视频与腕部表面肌电（sEMG）中估计物理手部状态，即联合恢复手部构型、接触状态与受力，输出3D手部姿态及手表面上的接触与法向力，目标是弥补仅凭外观无法区分接触与施力状态的不足。

Motivation: 现有工作依赖几乎相同的外观表象，无法区分不同的接触状态与实际施加的力，缺乏将视觉几何与神经肌肉证据联合建模的物理手部状态估计任务与配套数据。

Method: 构建EgoMyo基准（575万视频帧、50名参与者、34种接触构型、多次佩戴），将视频与sEMG和3D手部姿态、裸手压力标定的接触与法向力同步；控制实验表明视觉擅长空间结构、sEMG擅长力幅且增益集中于视觉最不可靠区域、接触定位限制力精度。据此提出MyoBridge：以手部区域为视觉-sEMG融合单元，各区域自门控sEMG权重，推理时仅将区域力幅分配到预测的接触顶点上。

实验效果: 在留出试次上，MyoBridge相较最强基线将姿态误差与力误差分别降低39.8%和18.5%，并在未见会话与受试者上取得最低姿态误差。

Abstract: Physical hand interaction depends on configuration, contact, and force, yet nearly identical appearances can conceal different contact states and exerted forces. We therefore formulate physical hand state estimation from egocentric video and wrist surface electromyography (sEMG), combining visual geometry with neuromuscular evidence to jointly recover these coupled components. To support this task, we introduce EgoMyo, a benchmark spanning 5.75M video frames, 50 participants, 34 contact configurations, and repeated donnings, synchronizing these inputs with 3D hand pose and with contact and normal force registered onto the hand surface from bare-hand pressure. Controlled analysis reveals that vision resolves spatial structure while sEMG better estimates force magnitude, that sEMG's gains concentrate in regions where vision is least reliable, and that contact localization limits force accuracy. Guided by these findings, we propose MyoBridge, which makes the hand region the unit of vision-sEMG fusion, lets each region gate its own share of sEMG, and distributes regional force magnitudes only over predicted contact vertices at inference. On held-out trials, MyoBridge reduces pose and force error by 39.8% and 18.5% over the strongest baseline and achieves the lowest pose error on unseen sessions and subjects.

</details>


### [153] [LiveRelight: Sub-Second Global Illumination Editing for Gaussian Interiors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34226)
*Noah Rothenberger,Dor Verbin,Serge Belongie,Bernhard Kerbl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种混合重光照方法，通过预计算几何光交换实现室内重建3D场景的近实时、高保真直接与间接光照编辑，无需逐次优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是对重建的室内3D场景进行重光照编辑：以重建场景（2D高斯与三角网格代理）及光源/材质编辑为输入，输出更新后的直接与间接光照效果，目标是让编辑者能以交互级速度将结果微调至满意。

Motivation: 现有重光照方法依赖缓慢或近似的视觉反馈，需逐次优化的路径追踪编辑无法提供近即时反馈，限制了编辑者反复微调、达到满意结果的能力。

Method: 核心思想是预计算几何光交换而非光照响应，从而保留一个廉价的传输求解以适配发光与表面反射率变化。具体地：在重建的2D高斯上，通过对单面发光体进行可见性感知采样计算直接光照；在三角代理上用稀疏辐射度传播漫反射互反射；再通过基于高斯alpha合成的稀疏投影将代理解迁移至重建结果；并通过重算光源注入支持快速灯具变换与发光体插入。

实验效果: 在合成测试集的8种重光照条件下，亚秒级编辑相比先前的路径追踪编辑平均提升5 dB PSNR，编辑延迟最多降低三个数量级；定性结果进一步展示了对真实采集场景的逼真交互式重光照。

Abstract: Relighting reconstructed 3D scenes remains challenging due to the complex nature of light transport: slow or approximate visual feedback restricts the editor's ability to fine-tune the results to satisfaction. We present a hybrid relighting approach that enables interactive, high-fidelity updates of direct and indirect indoor illumination without per-edit optimization. Our approach permits the editor to make a wide range of relighting edits at room-scale, with near-instant feedback. Our key insight is to precompute geometric light exchange rather than illumination responses, retaining an inexpensive transport solve that accommodates changes in emission and surface reflectance. We evaluate direct illumination on reconstructed 2D Gaussians using visibility-aware sampling of one-sided emitters, while propagating diffuse interreflection through sparse radiosity on a triangle proxy. A sparse projection fitted through Gaussian alpha compositing transfers the proxy solution to the reconstruction. Our formulation further supports fast fixture transformations and emitter insertion by recomputing light source injection. Across eight relighting conditions of our synthetic test suite, our sub-second edits improve relighting fidelity over prior path-traced editing by an average of 5\,dB PSNR, while reducing editing latency by up to three orders of magnitude. Qualitative results further demonstrate realistic, interactive relighting of real-world captures.

</details>


### [154] [OS-Omni: Benchmarking Generalist Computer-Using Agents on Long-Horizon Workflows Across Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34232)
*Hui Shen,Yunta Hsieh,Jianing Ma,Zhuoran Yin,Zhuhan Bao,Ziyuan Liu,Qi Han,Xiuqi Xu,Xianbang Wang,Wenhao Chai,Sihan Xu,Chenyang Zhao,Yanheng Shang,Dongfu Jiang,Rui Sun,Xingzhi Li,Chenqi Yin,Zesen Zhao,Boyuan Zheng,Jisen Li,Xiaoxia Wu,Ruoyan Zhang,Ruiyao Liu,Jing Xiong,He Xiao,Nan Zheng,Hangxiang Chen,Shengyang Tao,Deyang Luorong,Yifeng Liu,Jingxuan Zhang,Chaofan Tao,Yuren Wang,Jiaqi Mo,Rui Hu,Xing Wei,Xiaofeng Liu,Xin Xu,Han Wu,Mengyao Wen,Guobin Zhang,Shatong Zhu,Hei Ting Una Chan,Sicheng Chen,Kai Zhang,Mengkang Hu,Zhongwei Wan,Xin Wang,Chuanyang Zheng,Ziheng Zhang,Hengyuan Zhang,Zunhai Su,Kanzhi Cheng,Dehan Kong,Yian Wang,Hanrong Zhang,Jiawei Lu,Yifan Zhang,Junnan Li,Changyi Yang,Jintao Qu,Wenyao Gao,Jiaxuan Luo,Jiaxin Deng,Yuhao Chen,Xuesong Ye,Jingwen Gu,Yichi Zhang,Qun Lou,Shen Yan,Ben Athiwaratkun,Qiushi Sun,Mi Zhang,Ping Luo,Wenhu Chen,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 OS-Omni——一个覆盖 Windows、Ubuntu、macOS、iOS、Android 与 Web 的实时跨平台基准与执行环境，用于评估计算机使用型智能体在长时程、跨系统、状态持久化工作流上的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该工作面向计算机使用型智能体（computer-using agents），要求其完成真实用户目标而非孤立的 GUI 操作。基准以分波次（每波 300 个任务）发布，首版 v1 含每平台 48 个任务及 12 个桌面与 Android 手机配对的跨平台工作流，输出为可执行的最终状态评估结果与轨迹日志。

Motivation: 现有基准通常将 Web、移动端或桌面环境相互隔离，因而无法判断单一通用型智能体能否执行跨越多种操作系统与持久应用状态的长时程工作流。

Method: OS-Omni 构建实时跨平台执行环境，提供可复现的初始化、平台专用适配器、轨迹日志与可执行的最终状态评估器，并引入受控的服务后端应用（涉及账户、购物车、预算、日历、消息等持久状态），以支撑跨平台工作流的统一评测。

实验效果: 人类研究表明任务平均需 67.54 步交互，远超 OSWorld 的 15.0 步；在 v1 上最强被测智能体 Claude Opus 5.5 平均成功率 68.5%，落后人类参考 77.3% 达 8.8 个百分点，且差距分布不均——Linux 上超越人类，iOS 与 macOS 上落后超 25 个百分点。

Abstract: Computer-using agents are increasingly expected to complete realistic user goals rather than isolated GUI actions. However, existing benchmarks typically isolate web, mobile, or desktop environments, leaving it unclear whether a single generalist agent can execute long-horizon workflows that span multiple operating systems and persistent application states. We introduce OS-Omni, a live cross-platform benchmark and execution environment for evaluating computer-using agents across Windows, Ubuntu, macOS, iOS, Android, and Web environments. OS-Omni is released in versioned waves of 300 tasks each; this paper focuses on the first release, v1, which contains 48 tasks per platform and 12 cross-platform workflows that pair a desktop with an Android phone. OS-Omni provides reproducible initialization, platform-specific adapters, trajectory logging, and executable final-state evaluators, and includes controlled service-backed applications for workflows involving account, cart, budget, calendar, message, and other persistent state. Human studies show that OS-Omni tasks require 67.54 interaction steps, substantially exceeding OSWorld references of 15.0 steps. On v1, the strongest evaluated agent, Claude Opus 5.5, reaches 68.5% average success, trailing the 77.3% human reference by 8.8 points; the gap is uneven across platforms, with frontier models exceeding humans on Linux but trailing them by more than 25 points on iOS and macOS. These results indicate that current agents remain far from platform-robust, long-horizon computer use, especially when success requires sustained state tracking and durable final-state changes.

</details>


### [155] [VibeVideoAgent: Closing the Perceptual Loop for Code-Driven Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34237)
*Xuanyu Zheng,Luoyuan Zhang,Yancheng Long,Haoyang Xu,Muxi Diao,Yuzhe Chen,Kaiyu Jiang,Bin Wen,Tingting Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VibeVideoAgent 是一个免训练框架，让编码智能体在视频制作过程中通过独立的多模态感知反馈持续检查草稿，从而提升代码驱动视频生成的整体视听质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 代码驱动视频生成要求编码智能体编写并运行渲染程序，从而精确控制排版、数据可视化、音画同步与多镜头场景，并留下可编辑的工程文件；目标是在无运行时报错的情况下仍产出视听质量达标的成片。

Motivation: 程序正常退出并不代表渲染无误——标签重叠、镜头偏离主体、物体交互错误、旁白不同步等缺陷都不会触发运行时错误；而把渲染帧与音频直接回灌编码会话会膨胀长程上下文，仅对最终全分辨率成片做评审又会把早期设计错误拖成高昂返工。

Method: 框架将多模态感知放在与编码器隔离的短生命周期上下文中，仅返回凝练且有依据的 Findings；七个原语基于统一的"多时钟"资产与草稿表示，支持由粗到细的媒体探查、对引用需求逐模态取证核验、在不同时钟上比较草稿与参考或早期版本、以及局部测量流信号；轻量分阶段协议要求智能体先在最小预览上验证最高风险决策。

实验效果: 在全部 239 个 VibeVideoBench 任务上、匹配智能体运行时并使用固定感知模型时，整体视听质量 L3 分别从 4.03 提升至 4.43（Claude Opus 5.5）、从 4.03 提升至 4.27（GPT-6 Astra）、从 3.97 提升至 4.10（Claude Sonnet 5.5），L2 评分规范符合率由 98.87% 升至 99.35%，音画同步提升最大（至多 +0.56）；消融显示各组件均有贡献，轨迹审计表明代码编辑粒度决定了规范符合度与感知质量之间的权衡。

Abstract: Code-driven video generation asks a coding agent to write and run rendering programs. This gives exact control over typography, data visualizations, synchronized audio, and multi-shot scenes, and it leaves an editable project behind. A program that exits cleanly can still render overlapping labels, a camera aimed away from its subject, broken object interactions, or narration that drifts out of sync, and none of these raise a runtime error. Feeding rendered frames and audio back into the coding session bloats its long-horizon context, while critiquing only the finished full-resolution render turns early design mistakes into costly rewrites. We introduce VibeVideoAgent, a training-free framework that lets coding agents inspect their drafts throughout production. Multimodal perception runs in short-lived contexts kept apart from the coder and returns compact, grounded Findings. Seven primitives operate on a unified multi-clock representation of assets and drafts: they explore media from coarse to fine, verify quoted requirements with evidence from every required modality, compare a draft with a reference or an earlier revision on separate clocks, and measure stream signals locally. A lightweight staged protocol has the agent test its riskiest decision on a minimal preview first. On all 239 VibeVideoBench tasks, with matched agent runtimes and a fixed perception model, VibeVideoAgent improves holistic audiovisual quality (L3) for Claude Opus 5.5 (4.03 -> 4.43, while L2 rubric compliance rises from 98.87% to 99.35%), GPT-6 Astra (4.03 -> 4.27), and Claude Sonnet 5.5 (3.97 -> 4.10), with the largest gains in audio-visual synchronization (up to +0.56). Controlled ablations show that each component contributes, and trajectory audits show that the granularity of code edits governs the trade-off between rubric adherence and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [156] [Analemma $\infty$: A Temporal Benchmark for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34240)
*Alex López-Cifuentes,Laura Martínez-Ferrer,Juan Ignacio Bravo Pérez-Villar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Analemma——一个包含六个数据集、保留完整Sentinel-2全波段与生长季时间序列的时序基准，用统一冻结编码器协议评测时序基础模型并揭示评测设置对结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是对卫星影像时间序列进行像素级地表变化建模：模型以每个像元一整年的Sentinel-2观测为输入，输出土地覆盖/变化相关的预测。论文旨在提供带真实时间轴的公开基准，以公平衡量时序基础模型的能力。

Motivation: 现有公开基准缺乏时间维度，或仅抽样少数几个日期，无法体现"逐像元读取整年观测"的时序基础模型所依赖的完整时序信息；同时源文献中若干评测设置未被明确说明，可能导致比较结果不可靠。

Method: 构建Analemma：整合六个数据集，保留全部10 m与20 m Sentinel-2波段及覆盖整个生长季的时间序列，并在原始发布缺失几何、日期或云掩膜时予以补全；采用单一冻结编码器协议，在不同标签比例和三个随机种子下评测所有模型，并设置两个无预训练基线作对照；同时分析源文献未说明的三项评测选择对结果的影响。

实验效果: TESSERA总体排名第一；基于月度合成影像的从零训练模型除落后于OlmoEarth v1.3-Base外超过所有基础模型，说明时序输入本身解释了大部分性能。三项未说明的评测选择各使TESSERA v1的mIoU变动7.6–18.6，而前两名差距仅0.6–2.8。

Abstract: Satellite time series capture how land surfaces change over time. Pixel-wise temporal foundation models read a year of satellite observations per pixel, yet public benchmarks lack a temporal axis or subsample it to a few dates. We introduce Analemma, a temporal benchmark of six datasets that carry every 10 and 20 m Sentinel-2 band and a time series spanning a growing season, completed where their releases omit geometry, dates, or cloud masks. One frozen-encoder protocol evaluates all models across label ratios and three seeds, with two baselines without pretraining. The TESSERA model ranks first overall. A from-scratch model on monthly composites outranks every foundation model except OlmoEarth v1.3-Base, suggesting that temporal input explains most of the performance. Three evaluation choices left unstated by the source publications each shift TESSERA v1 by 7.6 to 18.6 mIoU, against top-two gaps of 0.6 to 2.8. Data, harness and leaderboard will be publicly released soon.

</details>


### [157] [Detector Operating Behavior Inference via Data-Space Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34252)
*Stefan Becker,Simon Weiss,Wolfgang Hübner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DOBI二级推理任务，从单张无标签图像的内部证据推断固定检测器在当前数据分布下的阈值相关运行行为（PR曲线），并用轻量监控器实现单图到行为状态的逆映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 检测器给定置信度阈值时的召回率与误报率依赖输入分布，因此需要估计检测器在当前数据条件下的分布级运行响应。DOBI任务的输入是一张无标签图像及固定检测器（YOLOv10-Nano）的内部证据，输出是跨阈值的召回、误报与精确率曲线（行为状态），目标是在线支持多种运行策略选择。

Motivation: 现有做法依赖在特定分布上离线标定阈值或按域查表，无法在缺乏标签和分布标识的部署条件下预知阈值响应随数据条件的变化；检索式查表也只能在已见离散域上有效，无法外推到未见条件组合。

Method: 将检测器的阈值响应视为当前数据条件下的潜在行为状态；先用受控的数据空间采样离线探测该状态空间，构建图像到行为状态的映射数据；再训练轻量监控器学习从单张图像内部证据到该状态的逆映射，其单一功能输出可支撑多种运行点策略。还在预设的连续与组合探测设置下考察功能解码器相对模板查表的外推能力。

实验效果: 在固定YOLOv10-Nano行人检测器与COCO衍生退化设定下，预测PR行为将相对图像无关均值先验的平均绝对偏差从0.128降至0.054，预测功能距离与经验工况距离的Spearman相关为0.96；单图推断选择的运行点在宏工况F1上与知晓真实工况的不可部署查表持平（0.6604对0.6604，原始标定0.6407，oracle 0.6620），但该离散基准上工况监督曲线查表是强对照；在未见组合上功能解码器平均曲线误差低于模板查表且随训练条件距离增大而优势扩大，而查表在插值情形更准。

Abstract: A detector scores candidate detections, but the recall and false alarms at a given confidence threshold depend on the input distribution. We introduce Detector Operating Behavior Inference (DOBI), a second-level inference task: estimating a fixed detector's threshold-dependent, distribution-level operating response from the internal evidence of a single unlabeled image. DOBI treats the detector's threshold response, i.e. recall, false positives, and precision across thresholds, as a latent behavioral state under the current data condition. Controlled data-space sampling probes this state space offline, and a lightweight monitor learns the inverse map from one image to this state, whose single functional output supports multiple operating policies. In a controlled proof of concept with a fixed YOLOv10-Nano person detector and COCO-derived degradations, predicted PR behavior reduces mean absolute deviation from 0.128 for an image-independent mean-behavior prior to 0.054, and predicted functional distances track empirical regime distances (Spearman 0.96). From one unlabeled image, the inferred behavior selects operating points as well as a non-deployable lookup that knows the true regime (macro-regime $F_1$ 0.6604 for both, versus 0.6407 under pristine calibration; oracle 0.6620). On this discrete benchmark, regime-supervised curve lookups are strong controls. Pre-specified continuous and compositional probing shows where functional inference matters: on unseen combinations, the functional decoder has a lower mean curve error than a template lookup, increasingly with distance from the training conditions, whereas the lookup is more accurate for interpolation. These results establish the feasibility of single-image inference of distribution-level detector behavior over the probed conditions. Source code is available at https://github.com/beckerio/dobi

</details>


### [158] [TORE: Token-Wise Taylor Order Selection via Decomposed Residual Error Estimation for Diffusion Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34261)
*Lei Tang,Baoquan Zhang,Yakun Zhang,Yunming Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TORE 通过估计全模型残差的预测误差来选择逐 token 的 Taylor 阶数，从而消除代理特征与预测目标不匹配的问题，实现扩散模型的高倍加速生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 扩散模型在图像与视频生成上表现优异，但迭代去噪带来显著的推理延迟。该任务旨在加速采样推理，输入为去噪过程中的残差序列，输出为各 token 在跳步中采用的 Taylor 预测阶数，目标是在保持生成质量与保真度的同时降低推理时延。

Motivation: 现有 token 自适应加速方法借助浅层代理分数为全模型残差挑选 Taylor 预测阶数，但阶数是在代理特征上评估、却用于预测全模型残差，形成"代理—目标不匹配"：代理偏好的阶数未必能最小化残差预测误差。

Method: TORE 基于全模型残差的估计预测误差进行逐 token 阶数选择。由于阶数选择时未来真值残差不可得，无法直接评估误差，TORE 转而分析 Taylor 外推中误差的成因，将预测误差分解为截断误差与有限差分导数误差，并利用已缓存的残差分别估计两部分贡献，据此为后续跳步预选各 token 的阶数。

实验效果: 在 FLUX.1-dev 上实现 4.31× 加速，在 Wan2.1 上实现 4.18× 加速，同时保持较高的生成质量与保真度。

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at image and video generation, but iterative denoising incurs substantial inference latency. Recent token-adaptive acceleration methods select Taylor prediction orders for the full-model residual using shallow-layer proxy scores. However, candidate orders are evaluated on these proxy features but used to predict the full-model residual, creating a proxy--target mismatch: the proxy-preferred order may not minimize residual prediction error. To address this mismatch, we propose \textbf{TORE} (\textbf{T}aylor \textbf{O}rder selection via \textbf{R}esidual \textbf{E}rror estimation), which selects token-wise Taylor orders based on estimated prediction errors for the full-model residual. Yet direct error evaluation requires future ground-truth residuals, which are unavailable during order selection. TORE therefore estimates prediction error by analyzing how errors arise in Taylor extrapolation. Specifically, it decomposes prediction error into truncation error and finite-difference derivative error, then estimates both contributions from cached residuals to preselect token-wise orders for upcoming skip steps. Extensive experiments show that TORE achieves $4.31\times$ speedup on FLUX.1-dev and $4.18\times$ on Wan2.1 while maintaining high generation quality and fidelity.

</details>


### [159] [Semantic State Reading for Joint Image-Semantic Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34279)
*Guanxi Lai,Zixuan Li,Zhiwei Li,Qi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出语义状态读取（SSR），在扩散Transformer中联合去噪图像潜变量、全局CLS token与紧凑patch状态，以不同读取路径实现全局—局部语义建模，显著加速训练并取得更优FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是基于预训练视觉表示训练扩散Transformer进行ImageNet 256×256图像生成，输入为图像潜变量及预训练语义状态，输出为去噪后的生成图像，目标是同时利用全局上下文与局部空间结构以提升生成质量与训练效率。

Motivation: 现有方法虽借助预训练视觉表示改进扩散训练，但需同时利用全局上下文与局部空间结构时，缺乏对“建模哪些语义状态”及“如何访问这些状态”的明确设计，导致语义信息在去噪过程中难以持续可用。

Method: SSR同时去噪图像潜变量、编码图像级上下文的全局CLS token和保持空间对应的紧凑patch状态，并采用两种读取路径：局部状态在输入端与对应图像token融合，全局状态经独立投影注入每个Transformer块，使两种语义范围在整个去噪过程中均可访问。

实验效果: 消融显示局部状态建模与重复全局读取具有互补收益，语义轨迹干预可在固定类别标签下改变图像布局；ImageNet 256×256上SiT-XL/2+SSR以分别少100×、40×、2×的训练迭代达到或超过SiT、REPA、REG的参考FID，2M迭代无引导FID为1.78，仅用CFG为1.34，CFG与autoguidance结合在1M迭代达到FID 1.31，为主对比表最佳。

Abstract: Pretrained visual representations improve diffusion-transformer training, yet exploiting both global context and local spatial structure requires deciding which semantic states to model and how to access them. We introduce Semantic State Reading (SSR), which jointly denoises image latents, a global CLS token encoding image-level context, and a compact patch state preserving spatial correspondence. SSR couples this global-local modeling with distinct reading paths: local states are fused with corresponding image tokens at the input, while independent projections inject the current global state into every transformer block. Both semantic scopes thus remain accessible throughout denoising. Ablations show complementary benefits from local-state modeling and repeated global reading, and semantic-trajectory interventions alter image layout under a fixed class label. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$, SiT-XL/2 with SSR reaches or surpasses the reference FIDs of SiT, REPA, and Representation Entanglement for Generation (REG) with $100\times$, $40\times$, and $2\times$ fewer training iterations, respectively. SSR achieves unguided FID 1.78 at 2M iterations and FID 1.34 with classifier-free guidance alone. Combining classifier-free guidance with autoguidance yields FID 1.31 at 1M iterations, achieving the best FID in our main comparison table.

</details>


### [160] [ReMeDi: Single-Image PBR Material Estimation via Reparameterization between Metal and Dielectric Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34284)
*Fan Fei,Xin Huang,Yixin Zhu,Jiawei Lu,Xin Yang,Jinglin Liang,Ruining Tang,Yifei Feng,Boxin Shi,Chunchao Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReMeDi 通过将 base color 重参数化为金属度加权的全金属/全介质混合，并用两个各自受限于单一假设的网络估计变体，使材质图中 base color 与金属度（intended metalness）保持物理一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是从图像估计 PBR 材质贴图（albedo/base color、metalness、roughness 等），用于重光照、材质编辑与 3D 内容创作；输入为图像，输出为可直接用于物理渲染的材质贴图，目标是提升材质准确性与重光照真实感。

Motivation: 金属度决定反照率是否应为高反射，若贴图与其“预期金属度”不一致（尤其在各贴图被独立预测的自动化方法中），会渲染出物理上不合理或语义被误读的材质；metallic–roughness 工作流虽显式给出金属度，但并不强制 base color 与之匹配。

Method: 提出 ReMeDi，含两部分：(1) 将 base color 重参数化为两个变体的金属度加权混合，两变体分别在全金属与全介质假设下估计，从而在结构上强制一致性；(2) 因现有监督信号无法让单一网络在每像素同时学习两种假设，改用两个独立网络各限定于一种假设，保证每个变体遵循其假设，同时改善各贴图本身的估计质量。

实验效果: 摘要报告实验表明 ReMeDi 在材质准确性与重光照保真度两方面均优于当前最优方法，但未给出具体数据集、指标数值或对比数字。

Abstract: Physically based rendering (PBR) material estimation facilitates relighting, material editing, and 3D content creation. As the reflectance of a surface heavily depends on whether it is a metal or a dielectric, producing any map that determines albedo involves a decision on metalness, which we call the "intended metalness" of the map. Rendering maps with mismatched intended metalness yields physically implausible or misinterpreted materials. The metallic--roughness workflow makes metalness explicit and thus encourages base color to match it, but does not enforce this match, especially for automated methods directly predicting each map. Therefore, we propose ReMeDi, which enforces the match through two components. First, we reparameterize base color as a metalness-weighted blend of its two variants, estimated under the all-metal and all-dielectric hypothesis, respectively. Second, as available supervision cannot teach a single network both hypotheses at every pixel, we restrict each of two separate networks to one hypothesis, so that each variant is ensured to follow its hypothesis. Besides enforcing the match, this also improves each estimated material map. Experiments show that ReMeDi outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both material accuracy and relighting fidelity.

</details>


### [161] [StateWeaver: Multi-Agent Coordination of Story-State Transitions for Long-Horizon Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34290)
*Modi Jin,Xuying Zhang,Yijing Guo,Haoran Li,Yanwen Ma,Jing Wang,Chenyu Ge,Ming-Ming Cheng,Haoyang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StateWeaver通过主动状态（Active States）与分层协作（Hierarchical Collaboration）机制，实现长视频跨镜头一致性与事件驱动状态变化的正确刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务为长视频生成：输入为预定义或自主规划的故事板（storyboard），输出为保持跨镜头一致性、并正确呈现事件驱动状态变化的长视频，目标是克服现有视频基础模型仅擅长短片段的问题。

Motivation: 现有智能体式（agentic）工作流因持续的规划与修复不断累积上下文，且参考（reference）更新能力受限，导致长视频一致性与状态变化刻画效果不佳。

Method: 提出StateWeaver：1）Active States将审查中识别的状态差异转化为具体视觉资产需求，指导参考构建、验证与再生成，在保留身份与已正确内容的同时修复目标属性；2）将确认的生成结果与资产状态、故事时间及参考版本绑定，供后续镜头验证与复用；3）Hierarchical Collaboration按任务、片段与修复轮次隔离执行上下文，并用分层记忆跨多集合组织该过程。

实验效果: 在多个基准上StateWeaver优于现有方法，在预定义与自主规划的故事板下均能生成具备持续跨镜头一致性的长视频；摘要未报告具体数值指标。

Abstract: Modern video foundation models generate high-quality short clips, but preserving long-video consistency and correctly depicting event-driven state changes remain challenging. Existing agentic workflows face these challenges because continual planning and repair accumulate context, while reference updates remain limited. We introduce StateWeaver, which combines Active States and Hierarchical Collaboration to connect review-driven visual reference updates with cross-shot state inheritance. Active States translate state discrepancies identified during review into concrete visual asset requirements that guide reference construction, validation, and regeneration, preserving identity and already-correct content while repairing target attributes. Confirmed generation results are linked to the corresponding asset states, story time, and reference versions for verification and reuse by subsequent shots. Hierarchical Collaboration isolates execution contexts by task, clip, and repair round, and uses hierarchical memory to organize this process across multiple sets. Across multiple benchmarks, StateWeaver outperforms existing methods and produces long videos with sustained cross-shot consistency under predefined and autonomously planned storyboards.

</details>


### [162] [WebVibe: Multimodal Interactive Website Generation via Reverse Synthesis and Visual Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34292)
*Tianxiang Jiang,Sheng Xia,Yilin Long,Kanghui Tian,Linquan Wu,Yongheng Zhang,Daixian Liu,Di Yin,Yi Wang,Yu Qiao,Qinglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 WebVibe 数据合成与 Interactive-SDPO 训练方法，构建 InteractiveCoder，实现从设计规格生成可交互多功能网站并在基准上大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是由设计规格自动生成具备多页面路由与有状态组件交互的功能性网站（输出可执行前端代码），目标是突破现有模型仅能将单张截图转译为静态 HTML 的局限。

Motivation: 现有训练流水线存在两处瓶颈：前向数据合成（X→Y）在代码执行前撰写规格 X，使 X 中未实现或被改动的需求成为监督噪声；强化学习中的标量奖励又丢弃了回放过程中观察到的大量状态转移信息。

Method: WebVibe 将合成方向反转为 Y→X，先在浏览器中验证跨页面与页内交互，再由回放确认的状态派生多模态输入，构建含 31,542 个站点、带可复用执行契约的 WebVibe-32K 语料；Interactive-SDPO 以回放轨迹和 2×2 状态转移拼图为条件，驱动权重共享的自教师模型提供 token 级监督，且不增加推理开销。

实验效果: 在公开 Vision2Web L2 上，一次性推理的 InteractiveCoder-9B 将基座模型功能分近乎提升三倍（20.2→55.2 FS），以总参数少 111 倍、token 少 78 倍的单次生成匹配 1T 参数编码智能体；在 WebVibeBench 上，4B 与 9B 版本均以超过 23 分的优势领先所有评测基线。

Abstract: Although multimodal large language models translate single screenshots into static HTML, generating interactive functional websites from design specifications still struggles with multi-page routing and stateful widget transitions. Examining existing training pipelines reveals two bottlenecks: (1) in forward data synthesis (X -> Y), writing the specification X before code execution leaves unimplemented or altered requirements in X as supervision noise; and (2) in reinforcement learning, scalar rewards discard the rich state transitions observed during replay. Based on these findings, we introduce WebVibe and Interactive-SDPO to train InteractiveCoder. WebVibe reverses synthesis into Y -> X by verifying cross-page and in-page interactions in the browser before deriving multimodal inputs from replay-confirmed states to build the 31,542-site WebVibe-32K corpus with reusable execution contracts. Interactive-SDPO then conditions a weight-sharing self-teacher on the replay trace and a 2x2 state-transition montage to provide token-level supervision at zero inference-time overhead. On the public Vision2Web L2, one-shot InteractiveCoder-9B nearly 3x its base model's functional score (20.2 to 55.2 FS) and matches a 1T-parameter coding agent in a single pass with 111x fewer total parameters and 78x fewer tokens. On our WebVibeBench, InteractiveCoder-4B and 9B further outperform all evaluated baselines by over 23 points.

</details>


### [163] [PostMesh: Self-Rewarded Preference Post-Training for Flow-Based Artist Mesh Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34319)
*Gihyun Kwon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PostMesh 是首个面向扩散/流式艺术家网格生成器的后训练方法，通过自采样与艺术网格奖励的多负样本流偏好优化，在无需人工真值的前提下显著提速并达到或超过自回归方法的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是将稠密三维物体转换为紧凑、以四边形为主、易于编辑的"艺术家网格"（artist mesh）。现有模型多为逐 token 自回归解码，单网格耗时数十至数百秒，因此目标是获得高质量且快速的生成器。

Motivation: 扩散与流式生成器速度远快于自回归方法，但其输出网格质量仍达不到艺术家水准；而直接依赖昂贵的艺术家手工真值进行训练成本过高。现有工作缺乏对快速生成器进行质量提升的后训练手段，这正是本文的切入点。

Method: 提出 PostMesh 后训练框架：(1) 生成器在自身采样结果上训练，无需艺术家真值；(2) 设计艺术网格奖励，基于稠密输入与输出计算，同时约束输出与稠密输入的一致性并将网格结构推向艺术化；(3) 提出多负样本流偏好优化（multi-negative flow preference optimisation），将奖励信号迁移至生成器；整体以短时 LoRA 后训练实现。

实验效果: 摘要报告：短时 LoRA 后训练后，PostMesh 在多数艺术家网格质量指标及盲测 VLM 偏好测试中与缓慢的自回归生成器相当或更优，且耗时仅为其一小部分（摘要未给出具体数值）。

Abstract: Artistic mesh generators turn a dense 3D object into an artist mesh: a compact, quad-dominant mesh that is easy to edit. Most of current models are auto-regressive methods which decode a mesh token by token, take tens to hundreds of seconds per mesh. Diffusion- and flow-based generators are much faster, but their meshes still fall short of artist quality. To our knowledge, PostMesh is the first post-training method for a diffusion- or flow-based artist mesh generator, and it needs no expensive artist-made ground truth: the generator is trained on its own samples, scored by a reward computed from the dense input and the output. We propose an artistic mesh reward that keeps the output consistent with the dense input while making the mesh structure into artistic. We also propose a multi-negative flow preference optimisation that transfers the reward to the generator. After a short LoRA post-training, PostMesh is comparable to or better than slow autoregressive generators on most artist-mesh quality metrics and in a blind VLM preference test, at a fraction of their time.

</details>


### [164] [SPARC: Semantic Calibration with Latent Multimodal Knowledge for Robust Image-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34344)
*Zhongkuan Mao,Wenzhuo Zhao,Haidong Chen,Shangze Li,Yidong Wang,Jinhe Bi,Yunpu Ma,Junfeng Fang,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARC 通过在球面切空间校准特征、融合隐式多模态知识与跨模态语义锚点，并施加软排序约束，提升不完整图像-文本查询下的候选区分度与排序稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 鲁棒图像-文本检索要求在图像被遮挡或文本词被删除等不完整查询下仍保持准确的跨模态匹配，以实现真实场景中可靠的信息获取；模型输入为不完整的图像/文本查询，输出为跨模态匹配与检索排序结果。

Motivation: 现有方法主要聚焦于适应分布偏移，未充分解决不完整查询下的候选区分度不足以及排序不稳定问题。

Method: SPARC 从冻结编码器中提取隐式多模态知识，包含三个模块：(1) 语义校准模块在球面乘积空间上通过切空间更新精炼不完整的视觉与文本特征，保持用于余弦相似度的单位球面结构；(2) 鲁棒语义引导模块结合完整图文特征中的隐式多模态知识与跨模态语义锚点，促进校准后特征的语义对齐；(3) 匹配关系约束模块融合原始查询特征与校准特征，并施加软排序约束惩罚初始正匹配边距的缩减，从而限制排序扰动。

实验效果: 在 Flickr30K 与 COCO 上的大量实验表明，SPARC 在多种图像遮挡与词删除设置下优于对比方法，并在不同预训练骨干网络上取得一致增益；摘要未报告具体数值。

Abstract: Robust image-text retrieval aims to maintain accurate cross-modal matching when image or text queries are incomplete, supporting reliable information access in real-world applications. However, existing methods typically focus on adapting to distribution shifts, without adequately addressing candidate discrimination for incomplete queries and ranking instability. To this end, we propose semantic projection and alignment with relational constraints (SPARC), which elicits latent multimodal knowledge from frozen encoders to improve candidate discrimination and ranking stability. Specifically, we first devise a semantic calibration module on a product of spheres that refines incomplete visual and textual features through tangent-space updates, maintaining the unit-sphere structure used for cosine similarity. Second, we introduce a robust semantic guidance module that combines latent multimodal knowledge encoded in complete image-text features with cross-modal semantic anchors to promote semantic alignment of the calibrated features. Finally, we introduce a matching relation constraint module that fuses original query features with their calibrated counterparts and applies a soft ranking constraint to penalize reductions in initially positive matching margins, thereby limiting ranking perturbations. Extensive experiments on Flickr30K and COCO demonstrate that SPARC outperforms competing methods under various image occlusion and word deletion settings, while achieving consistent gains across different pretrained backbones.

</details>


### [165] [AIRIS: Benchmarking Social Embodied Intelligence in Egocentric Streaming Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34347)
*Yuchen Zhou,Hongsheng Wu,Jiayu Tang,Yunxuan Mao,Hanjing Ye,Shenghao Zhang,Jing Tang,Chun-Kai Fan,Yufei Huang,Yonggen Ling,He Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AIRIS 是一个因果流式基准，将社交具身智能建模为时间锚定的注意—意图—反应—交互（A-I-R-I）链条，用于诊断在线社交决策的失败环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 论文面向在人群中运行的具身智能体，要求其在仅可获得当前观察前缀的条件下，判断现有证据是否充分、是否应当介入，并以不违背社会规范的方式作出响应。基准输入为第一视角（egocentric）视频的流式观测前缀，输出为对应时间锚点上的判断与反应，目标是评估从被动社交感知到可靠具身交互的能力。

Motivation: 现有基准通常在孤立设置下评估这些能力，或允许模型访问完整视频，因而掩盖了在线社交决策究竟在何处失败；同时缺乏对“等待时机”与“过早表态”这类时间校准问题的显式度量。

Method: 作者构建 AIRIS：将社交具身智能形式化为 Attention、Intention、Reaction、Interaction 四阶段的时间锚定因果链条，基于 357 段第一视角片段（覆盖 11 类情境、36 个能力维度）由专家筛选出 8,000 个问题。每个查询仅向模型提供该时刻可获得的观测前缀，并采用决策感知指标，奖励校准得当的等待与完整反应、惩罚过早承诺，从而定位失败环节。

实验效果: 在 45 个专有与开源模型（通用、具身、流式视频、全模态）上的评测显示能力分布碎片化且时间校准失准：通用 VLM 在被动线索定位与意图识别上占优，具身专用模型在孤立的反应子决策上领先；但所有模型家族均常在证据不足时猜测意图，且难以在同一回合内同时对齐介入时机、动作域与具体执行。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating around people must not only recognize social cues, but also decide when the available evidence is sufficient, whether to intervene, and how to respond without violating social norms. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these abilities in isolation or with access to completed videos, obscuring where an online social decision fails. We introduce AIRIS, a causal streaming benchmark that formulates social embodied intelligence as a time-anchored chain of Attention, Intention, Reaction, and Interaction (A-I-R-I). AIRIS contains 8,000 expert-curated questions grounded in 357 egocentric clips across 11 context categories and 36 capability dimensions. At each query, models receive only the observation prefix available at that time; decision-aware metrics reward calibrated waiting and complete reactions while penalizing premature commitment. Evaluating 45 proprietary and open-source general-purpose, embodied, streaming-video, and omni-modal models reveals fragmented and temporally miscalibrated competence. General-purpose VLMs excel at passive cue grounding and intent recognition, whereas embodied specialists lead on isolated reaction sub-decisions; yet across all families, models frequently guess intents before sufficient evidence emerges and fail to jointly align intervention timing, action domain, and concrete execution within the same episode. AIRIS thus provides a diagnostic foundation for bridging the gap between passive social perception and reliable embodied interaction.

</details>


### [166] [One Patch, Many Policies: Object-Associated Physical Patch Attacks on Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34360)
*Yiqian Liu,Yuqi Lin,Hui Lu,Jiaming He,Qi Zhu,Deyi Ji,Bing Li,Kaizheng Wang,Zhiyuan Zha,Yi Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出对象关联补丁攻击（OAPA），一个通用且可迁移的物理补丁框架，可通过补丁放置位置选择性地破坏攻击者指定任务物体的操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 针对视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）与世界动作模型（WAM）的对抗鲁棒性研究，输入为物理场景中的对抗补丁与相机视角，输出为使模型动作发生偏离的扰动补丁，目标是实现通用、可跨任务与跨策略迁移的物理补丁攻击。

Motivation: 现有攻击多针对单一模型或需针对每个目标重新优化，缺乏通用性与可迁移性，且无法在物理部署中控制具体干扰哪一任务物体的操作。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：对象关联的补丁放置（由放置位置决定被干扰的任务物体）、考虑多相机视角下透视与遮挡的可微物理合成、以及以最终动作专家表征散度（相对RMS或余弦距离）作为优化目标的补丁优化，从而无需重新优化即可复用同一补丁。

实验效果: 摘要定性报告了跨任务套件与跨策略的强攻击可迁移性，以及通过补丁放置选择性破坏攻击者所选任务物体操作的能力；未报告具体基准数值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World Action Models (WAMs) are vulnerable to adversarial visual interference, motivating the study of universal and transferable physical patch attacks. We propose Object-Associated Patch Attack (OAPA), a universal and transferable physical patch framework that enables an attacker to select which task object's manipulation to disrupt through patch placement, without re-optimizing the patch. Our framework combines object-associated placement, differentiable physical compositing, and final action-expert representation divergence. Physical compositing accounts for perspective and occlusion across camera views, while representation divergence guides patch optimization using either relative RMS or cosine distance. Experimental results demonstrate strong attack transferability across task suites and policies, together with the ability to selectively disrupt manipulation of an attacker-chosen task object through patch placement.

</details>


### [167] [MoCA4D: Monocular 3D Bounding Box Lifting and Tracking with Camera and Object Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34370)
*Changwoo Jeon,Jongyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoCA4D 通过双分支分解相机运动与物体刚性运动，并结合 Star Optimization 迭代优化，实现单目视频中更精确的 3D 框提升与跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 该任务是从单目视频、关联的 2D 框提示和相机内参出发，输出逐帧 3D 边界框并实现跟踪，目标是同时处理物体运动与相机视角变化带来的影响。

Motivation: 现有逐帧框提升加时序滤波的方法将运动成分隐式处理，而静态场景融合又假设物体静止，二者都无法显式建模相机运动与物体自身运动的分离。

Method: MoCA4D 包含观测分支（估计逐帧几何）与动态分支（预测所有物体共享的相机变换及各物体独立的刚性变换）；提出 Star Optimization，利用观测框与经上述变换传播的历史框联合精化当前深度与朝向，精化后的框再指导后续传播，形成迭代闭环。

实验效果: 在 Argoverse 2 与 ADT 上，Star Optimization 相较对相同观测做卡尔曼滤波，中心运动误差分别降低 38.1% 和 38.7%；在 MOVi-F 的八次转移运动窗口上，动态分支取得最低的角点误差与旋转误差及最高 IoU；真实数据纯相机对照进一步表明预测物体运动可改善动态轨迹上的定位。

Abstract: Monocular 3D bounding box tracking must account for both object motion and changes in camera viewpoint. Framewise box lifting with temporal filtering leaves these motion components implicit, while static-scene fusion assumes stationary objects. We introduce MoCA4D, a framework for 3D box lifting and tracking from monocular video, associated 2D box prompts, and camera intrinsics. An observation branch estimates per-frame geometry, while a dynamic branch predicts a camera transformation shared across objects and a separate rigid transformation for each object. We propose Star Optimization to refine current depth and orientation using the observation and historical boxes propagated through these transformations. The refined box then guides subsequent propagation. Across all evaluated objects in Argoverse 2 and ADT, Star Optimization reduces center motion error by 38.1% and 38.7%, respectively, relative to Kalman filtering of the same observations. On MOVi-F, the dynamic branch achieves the lowest corner and rotation errors and the highest IoU among the evaluated methods over eight-transition motion windows. Real-data camera-only controls further link predicted object motion to improved localization on dynamic-object tracks.

</details>


### [168] [Plausible Is Not Proven: Testing Failure Explanations in Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34393)
*Zeeshan Ali,Md Rayyan,Anshuman Chakravorty,Omer Faraz,Anshi Bajaj,Amit Saini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Vision Error Observatory (VEO) 审计框架，对单目深度模型的失败区域归因标签进行四项检验，发现多数看似合理的失败解释经不起验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是对单目深度估计模型的失败区域（未通过 δ1 判据的传感器实测像素）给出成因标签，并在 NYU Depth V2、DIODE、iBims-1（另含 Hypersim 验证）上对八个模型进行系统审计，最终输出可复现的协议、代码、评分与结果文件。

Motivation: 现有失败分析通常直接为每个错误指定成因（如遮挡边界、细薄物体、反射面），但这些标签本身很少被检验，其可靠性缺乏证据支撑。

Method: VEO 先定位未通过 δ1 的区域并为每个区域标注可能成因或选择弃答，随后用四项测试验证标签：与未见数据上物体标注的一致性、与盲评人类评分者的一致性、对定向图像编辑的响应性，以及在微调中作为训练信号的价值；多数判定规则在结果产生前即固定。

实验效果: 遮挡边界标签与标注一致性较好（NYU AUROC 0.85、Hypersim 0.81、iBims-1 0.69），但单一深度边缘线索表现相当，且最强模型的多数边界失败在 2 像素容差下消失；反射标签无法迁移到合成数据，无纹理标签接近随机，四项编辑测试仅一项探索性测试支持其标签；与五位盲评者共识仅为随机水平（Cohen's κ=0.05），提高诊断区域权重使目标像素误差在每个测试权重下均上升，先前宣称的架构特异性失败指纹也未能跨数据集复现。

Abstract: Failure analyses of monocular depth models often name a cause for each error, such as an occluding boundary, a thin object or a reflective surface, but such labels are rarely tested. We present the Vision Error Observatory (VEO), an audit that finds regions failing the $δ_1$ criterion on sensor-measured pixels, labels each with a likely cause or abstains, and then subjects the labels to four tests: agreement with object annotations on data the label model never saw, agreement with blinded human raters, response to targeted image edits, and value as a training signal. We apply it to eight models on NYU Depth V2, DIODE and iBims-1, with labels also checked on Hypersim, and fix most decision rules before their results. The occluding-boundary label agrees with annotations (AUROC 0.85 on NYU, 0.81 on Hypersim, 0.69 on iBims-1), but a single depth-edge cue does as well, and most boundary failures of the strongest model vanish under a 2-pixel tolerance. The reflection label does not transfer to synthetic data, the textureless label is near chance against annotations, and only one of four edit tests, an exploratory one, supports its label. Evaluated against five blinded raters, VEO's labels matched the human consensus only at chance level (Cohen's $κ$ = 0.05). In addition, an increase in the weight on the diagnosed regions during fine-tuning led to an increase in the error on targeted pixels at every weight tested. The architecture-specific failure fingerprint claimed by an earlier version of this project also failed to replicate across the datasets. Plausible failure explanations, in short, need to be tested before they are trusted; we release the protocol, code, ratings and every result file.

</details>


### [169] [SemProxy: Semantic 3D Proxies for Controllable Agentic 3D Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34414)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Changjian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemProxy 提出以带标签的三维包围盒作为轻量中间表示，让用户直接指定物体部件与空间布局，把几何细节留给编码智能体，并配套 ProxyStudio 交互界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 论文面向"由编码智能体生成三维模型"的人机协同建模任务：用户需要控制建模过程与空间意图，系统据此输出三维模型；目标是实现直观、可控、少迭代的建模交互。

Motivation: 现有工作流主要依赖文本提示与参考图像，难以表达精确的空间意图，往往需要反复修正，缺乏可直接编辑空间结构的控制手段。

Method: 以"语义三维代理"（labeled 3D bounding boxes）作为中间表示：用户编辑包围盒来指定部件与空间排布，几何细节交由编码智能体生成；代理可序列化为结构化文本并渲染为图像，从而无需额外训练或修改即可被现有编码智能体使用；并构建 ProxyStudio 界面，整合文本、图像参考与交互式三维代理编辑。

实验效果: 摘要未报告定量实验结果，仅给出定性证据：在物体级与场景级建模任务上、配合不同编码智能体验证了 SemProxy 的通用性，并通过用户研究评估其在支持可控人机协同三维建模方面的有效性。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly capable of generating 3D models, yet enabling users to effectively control the modeling process remains challenging. Existing workflows rely primarily on text prompts and reference images, which often struggle to convey precise spatial intentions and require iterative corrections. We introduce SemProxy, a lightweight intermediate representation that bridges intuitive human interaction and agentic 3D modeling. Instantiated as labeled 3D bounding boxes, semantic 3D proxies allow users to directly specify object parts and spatial arrangements while leaving geometric details to coding agents. These proxies can be serialized into structured text and rendered as images, making them directly accessible to existing coding agents without additional training or modifications. We further develop ProxyStudio, a user interface that integrates text, image references, and interactive 3D proxy editing into agentic modeling workflows. We demonstrate the versatility of SemProxy across object- and scene-level modeling tasks with different coding agents, and evaluate its effectiveness in supporting controllable human-agent collaborative 3D modeling through a user study.

</details>


### [170] [SPGSR: A Small Pixel-Space Generator for One-Step Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34418)
*Yun Yue,Luming Liang,Yiqi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPGSR 通过在低分辨率图像上叠加学习到的 RGB 修正，并用冻结 DINOv2 引导，以仅 31.8M 参数的生成器实现优于 VOSR 的单步超分质量与显著提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 单步超分辨率任务中，模型需从低分辨率（LR）图像直接预测高分辨率（HR）输出，并保持语义线索与待恢复结构的空间对齐；目标是高质量、高效率地完成恢复。

Motivation: 现有视觉单模态 SR 模型虽性能优异，但生成器规模可高达 14 亿参数；而 LR 图像本身已提供大量结构信息，因此需要验证远小于现有规模的生成器能否达到相当质量。

Method: 提出 SPGSR 视觉单模态框架：以一个冻结的 DINOv2 模型作为语义引导，在 LR 图像上预测一个学习到的 RGB 修正，并用轻量级模块对修正进行局部细化，从而以极小生成器完成超分。

实验效果: 在三个基准上，SPGSR 的 PSNR/SSIM 高于、LPIPS/DISTS 低于领先的视觉单模态方法 VOSR，且与基于 T2I 的 SR 方法质量相当；其 31.8M 参数生成器仅为 VOSR-0.5B 的 6.5%，训练所用 HR 像素仅占 VOSR 生成器训练与蒸馏用量的 0.60%；512×512 输出下相对 VOSR-0.5B 与 VOSR-1.4B 分别实现 2.80× 与 3.35× 的 GPU 流水线加速。

Abstract: In one-step super-resolution (SR), vision-only models extract semantic cues directly from the low-resolution (LR) image, keeping those cues spatially aligned with the structures they help restore. Recent models achieve high restoration quality with generators of up to 1.4 billion parameters. Given how much structure the LR image already provides, could a much smaller generator achieve comparable quality? We introduce SPGSR, a vision-only framework that adds a learned RGB correction to the LR image. A frozen DINOv2 guides prediction, and a lightweight block refines the correction locally. SPGSR achieves higher PSNR/SSIM and lower LPIPS/DISTS than VOSR, a leading vision-only SR method, across three benchmarks. Its $31.8\mathrm{M}$-parameter generator is just $6.5\%$ the size of VOSR-0.5B's. SPGSR also achieves competitive quality against T2I-based SR methods. SPGSR's generator uses only $0.60\%$ of the HR pixels used to train and distill VOSR's generator. For $512\times512$ outputs, SPGSR achieves $2.80\times$ and $3.35\times$ GPU-pipeline speedups over VOSR-0.5B and VOSR-1.4B, respectively.

</details>


### [171] [DocSEAM: Bidirectional Cross-Page Encoding for Multi-Page Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34420)
*Hoang-Nhat Nguyen,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量模块DocSEAM，在视觉编码器与语言模型之间注入跨页局部上下文，缓解多页文档模型中页面边界处的特征不连续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 多页文档视觉问答任务中，模型需以多页图像为输入，跨页整合信息并输出答案；目标是克服逐页独立编码带来的跨页信息割裂。

Motivation: 现有多页文档VLM逐页独立编码，视觉token在生成时无法访问相邻页内容，语言模型只能基于孤立形成的页级表示推理，导致页面边界处出现特征层面的不连续。

Method: DocSEAM插入预训练视觉编码器与语言模型之间：对每页与其左右邻页构成局部三元组，采用中心锚定的掩码自注意力并配合方向显著性门控融合邻页信息，仅输出增强后的中心页token以保持token数不变；训练上先做有监督跨页预热，再在多页微调阶段冻结DocSEAM。

实验效果: 9B参数模型在五个多页文档VQA基准上取得所评估的10B以下开放权重模型中的SOTA；在受控重分页实验中，DocSEAM降低了模型对边界位置的敏感性并提升了跨页片段定位能力。

Abstract: Multi-page document vision-language models encode each page independently, creating a feature-level discontinuity at page boundaries: visual tokens are formed without access to content on adjacent pages, and the language model must reason over these page representations formed in isolation. We introduce DocSEAM (Document Selective Enrichment via Anchored Masking), a lightweight module inserted between a pretrained vision encoder and the language model that injects the inductive bias of local cross-page context into visual representations. For each page, DocSEAM forms a local triplet with its two neighbors, applies center-anchored masked self-attention with directional salience gates to fuse information from neighbor pages, and returns only the enriched center-page tokens, leaving the visual token count unchanged. After a supervised cross-page warm-up and multi-page fine-tuning with DocSEAM frozen, our 9B-parameter model achieves state-of-the-art results among the evaluated open-weight models of up to 10B parameters on five multi-page document VQA benchmarks. We further show that under controlled re-pagination, DocSEAM reduces sensitivity to boundary placement and improves cross-page span localization

</details>


### [172] [Can Visual World Models See Force?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34422)
*Felix Tristram,Benjamin D. Killeen,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文定义并探查视觉基础模型表征中的"力感知"(force awareness)，发现多数模型只能感知接触是否发生，却无法表征力的大小，且机器人后训练也无改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 面向机器人操作的世界模型与视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略模型通常预测场景演化与动作，但成功操作还依赖不可观测的交互力。本文以同步视频与力测量数据为输入，用轻量读出探针评估冻结表征，目标是判断视觉基础模型表征是否编码交互力信息。

Motivation: 现有世界模型与策略模型仅以可观测轨迹为导向，将交互力这一隐藏变量排除在表征之外；尽管神经生理学表明人类具备对该隐藏变量的视觉先验，但视觉基础模型是否同样具备并未被检验，这一能力缺口缺乏系统量化。

Method: 将力感知拆解为三项可测能力：(1)判断接触力是否施加，(2)追踪交互过程中力的变化，(3)反映力的大小。对冻结的图像、视频、世界模型与策略模型表征训练轻量读出层，使用两个操作数据集中的同步视频与力测量进行探针评估。

实验效果: 实验表明多数模型能捕捉接触何时发生，但无法表征所施加力的大小；令人意外的是，机器人后训练也未改善该能力，揭示了当前模型能力上的重要缺口(摘要未报告具体数值或基准对比)。

Abstract: World models and vision-language-action policy models are increasingly proposed as foundations for robot manipulation. They predict how scenes evolve and which actions to take, yet successful manipulation depends not solely on following observable trajectories but also on the hidden variable of interaction force. Neurophysiology has shown that humans possess a visual prior for this hidden variable, motivating us to ask the question: Do the representations of visual foundation models also capture the forces involved in an interaction? We define force awareness as the ability of representations to (1) indicate when contact force is applied, (2) track how it changes during an interaction, and (3) reflect how large it is. We probe frozen image, video, world- and policy model representations with a lightweight readout trained on synchronized video and force measurements from two manipulation datasets. Our experiments show that most models capture when contact occurs but not how much force is being applied. Surprisingly, robot post-training does not improve this either, highlighting an important gap in current models capabilities.

</details>


### [173] [One Representation to Rule Them All -- Open-Vocabulary Change Detection from Text or Visual Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34430)
*Blaž Rolih,Matic Fučka,Filip Wolf,Luka Čehovin Zajc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OVTSID3 是一个免训练的统一框架，在 DINOv3 表示空间中整合区域分组、概念识别与时序比较，支持文本零样本与视觉单样本两种概念指定方式，实现开放词汇变化检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）的目标是在不针对预定义类别重新训练的前提下，定位用户指定的变化。其输入为双时相影像及用户给定的目标概念，输出为对应变化区域。

Motivation: 现有方法依赖文本描述来表达目标概念，并将区域提议、语义识别与时序比较拆分为多个独立模型，既限制了概念的表达方式，也使检测结果受制于多个中间预测的级联误差。

Method: 提出免训练框架 OVTSID3，在 DINOv3 的表示空间内统一完成区域分组、概念识别与时序比较；概念指定支持两种互补模式——零样本文本查询，以及通过带掩膜的参考影像对进行单样本视觉提示。

实验效果: 在两个建筑类与两个自然灾害类基准上，OVTSID3 优于所有此前的免训练 OVCD 方法，平均 F1 提升 8.9 个点；同时支持语义变化检测，并在 SECOND 上取得最优性能。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary change detection (OVCD) aims to localise user-specified changes without retraining for predefined categories. Existing approaches typically rely on textual descriptions and combine separate models for region proposal, semantic recognition, and temporal comparison. This limits how target concepts can be expressed and makes detection dependent on several intermediate predictions. We introduce OVTSID3, a training-free framework that unifies region grouping, concept identification, and temporal comparison in the representation space of DINOv3. OVTSID3 supports two complementary modes of concept specification: zero-shot textual queries or one-shot visual prompting through a masked reference pair. OVTSID3 outperforms all prior training-free OVCD methods on two building and two natural-disaster benchmarks, raising the average F1 by 8.9 points. OVTSID3 also supports semantic change detection and achieves state-of-the-art on SECOND. These results demonstrate the flexibility of a unified representation for text- or example-guided change detection without task-specific model training.

</details>


### [174] [Most of the Way There: Turning a Strong Video Prior into an Action-Conditioned World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34435)
*Zanyi Wang,Xia Chen,Liu Liu,Xianglong Guo,Dengyang Jiang,Haodong Li,Tingyang Xiao,Wenhao Hu,Zhizhong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Dianjing 以极少的参数微调将强视频先验转化为动作条件世界模型，验证了强视频先验已接近世界建模所需的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 动作条件世界模型（ACWM）旨在模拟世界对机器人动作的响应，从而在无需昂贵的真实环境 rollout 的情况下实现可扩展的策略评估与改进；其输入为机器人动作（及历史/图像条件），输出为动作后续的物理合理未来视频预测。

Motivation: 现有视频生成器侧重语义合理性，无法保证对特定动作物理后果的保真；而构建 ACWM 的难点不只是生成合理未来，而是预测某一具体物理动作会导致什么后果。作者据此追问：在视频先验日益强大时，究竟还需多少适配。

Method: 提出 Dianjing 最小化配方：统一物理动作条件注入、将训练集中于决定运动与布局（即动作后果）的高噪声水平区间、原生历史条件；基于 33B 视频基础模型 MiniMax H3，冻结预训练主干，仅训练 0.34% 参数，使用约 65 小时、两种本体机器人视频，无需额外机器人专用预训练或相机标定。

实验效果: 对照消融表明，训练在噪声水平上的分配比动作注入方式更重要；在 WorldOracle（以配对真实执行评估预测）上，Dianjing 在五个评测赛道中四个取得最佳成绩，在 AgiBot-Long 上排名第二。

Abstract: Modern video generators excel at producing visually plausible futures guided by text and image prompts. Yet their emphasis on semantic plausibility does not guarantee fidelity to the physical consequences of specific actions. Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs), in contrast, aim to simulate how the world responds to robot actions, enabling scalable policy evaluation and improvement without costly real-world rollouts. The challenge is not merely to generate a plausible future, but to predict what follows from a specific physical action. As video priors grow stronger, we ask how little adaptation is truly needed to bridge this gap. We introduce Dianjing, a minimal recipe that preserves and unlocks the capabilities of strong video priors through unified physical action conditioning, training concentrated at the high noise levels where motion and layout, and thus the consequences of actions, are decided, and native history conditioning. Built upon MiniMax H3, a 33B video foundation model, Dianjing freezes the pretrained backbone and trains only 0.34% of its parameters using approximately 65 hours of robot video across two embodiments, without additional robot-specific pretraining or camera calibration. Controlled ablations show that where training is allocated across noise levels matters more than how actions enter the network.
