### [241] [Tracking Any Point with Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34943)
*Xuweiyi Chen,Jin Yao,Linzhan Mou,Lei Gong,Di Liu,Cheng Zhang,Ziqiao Ma,Sihan Xu,Adam W. Harley,Shengyi Qian,Zezhou Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于VLM的智能体AgenTAP，通过主动推理视频直接预测点坐标与可见性，在无需对应关系微调的情况下超越专家视觉模型，并用于构建长时第一人称视频3D点轨迹数据集EgoTAP-4D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 点对应任务要求跨观测对同一物理表面点进行像素级定位，即使外观变化或点消失后重现也需正确识别。本文以视频为输入，输出点的坐标与可见性判断，目标是实现稳健的点级对应与跟踪。

Motivation: 现有通用VLM虽在目标检测、视频识别等目标级任务上超过专家模型，但点对应超出目标级识别范畴，涉及像素级定位与遮挡/消失后重现的判别，因而对VLM构成显著挑战。

Method: 构建VLM智能体AgenTAP：从自身初始估计出发，自主决定检查哪些区域或帧，利用所获证据验证点的身份并迭代精化预测；同时直接预测点坐标与可见性，无需针对对应任务的微调。作者进一步用其重识别点消失后的物理点、拼接断裂轨迹，构建EgoTAP-4D数据集。

实验效果: 使用GPT-6 Astra时，AgenTAP在点定位与可见性预测上均超越专家视觉模型且无需对应关系微调；在采样重现点上其预测接近人类一致性水平，将EgoTAP-4D加入D4RT训练可提升平均3D跟踪性能。摘要未给出具体数值指标。

Abstract: A general-purpose Vision-Language Model (VLM) has surpassed expert vision models in many domains such as object detection and video recognition. However, point correspondence is known to be challenging for VLMs since it goes beyond object-level recognition: it requires pixel-level localization of the same physical surface point across observations, even when appearance changes or the point disappears and later reappears. In this paper, we introduce AgenTAP, a VLM-based agent that directly predicts point coordinates and visibility and actively reasons over videos. Starting from its own estimate, the agent autonomously chooses what to inspect and uses the resulting evidence to verify point identity and refine its predictions. With GPT-6 Astra, it surpasses expert vision models in both point localization and visibility prediction, without correspondence-specific fine-tuning. We further use AgenTAP to re-identify physical points after disappearance and reconnect fragmented tracks, constructing EgoTAP-4D, a dataset of 3D point trajectories in long real-world egocentric videos. On sampled reappearing points, its predictions approach human agreement, and adding EgoTAP-4D to D4RT training improves average 3D tracking performance.

</details>


### [242] [AnyCam: Anything Can Represent Camera for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34944)
*Weijie Lyu,Zhixin Shu,Yuntian Ke,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyCam 以场景中的锚定物体作为相机表示，通过重建并沿目标轨迹渲染该物体来为视频扩散模型提供尺度明确、视角完整的相机信号，实现环绕物体的相机可控视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是相机可控的视频生成：给定一段源视频和目标相机轨迹，重新渲染出沿新轨迹的同一场景视频，尤其在物体中心的场景中实现绕物体的视角变换，输出为内容与动态保持一致的新视角视频。

Motivation: 现有方法或仅以相机参数为条件（在单目视频中尺度不确定），或依赖扭曲点云、深度等显式几何（在大视角变化下失效）；因此绕物体大幅移动相机这一最自然的视角变换方式仍难以实现。

Method: 提出用场景中的锚定物体来表示相机：先对锚定物体做三维重建，再沿目标轨迹渲染，得到尺度感知、视角完整且易于相对物体指定的相机信号；视频扩散模型负责将源视频的内容与动态迁移至新视角，其 H-Concat 布局在时间上对齐源帧与目标帧，从而可蒸馏出流式少步生成器 AnyCam-AR 以支持即时相机控制；模型在多相机视频与静态场景采集得到的像素对齐锚定渲染配对数据上训练。

实验效果: 摘要报告了在多种数据集与轨迹上的实验，称 AnyCam 在相机控制与视觉质量上优于相机可控视频生成方法与三维重建方法，但未给出具体指标数值。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation re-renders a video along a target camera trajectory, yet moving the camera around an object, the most natural way to change the viewpoint of an object-centric scene, remains difficult. Existing methods condition either on camera parameters, which are scale-ambiguous in monocular video, or on explicit geometry such as warped point clouds or depth, which breaks down under large viewpoint changes. We present AnyCam, a method that represents the camera with an anchor object in the scene: we reconstruct it in 3D and render it along the target trajectory, a camera signal that is scale-aware, view-complete and easy to specify relative to the object. A video diffusion model transfers the content and dynamics of the source video to the new viewpoint. Its H-Concat layout aligns source and target frames in time, so the model can be distilled into AnyCam-AR, a streaming few-step generator for on-the-fly camera control. Both models are trained on paired videos with pixel-aligned anchor renderings from multi-camera videos and static-scene captures. Experiments on diverse datasets and trajectories show that AnyCam performs favorably against camera-controlled video generation and 3D reconstruction methods in camera control and visual quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [Real-time Food Recipe Generation with Mobile Deep Learning and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33177)
*Wei Jun Leung,Mun Hong Lee,Joo Wee Quek,Desmond Loy,Yifei Wang,Yan Ning Ang,Pai Chet Ng,Kan Chen,Jeannie Lee*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 该论文开发了一款移动应用，通过相机识别食材并结合深度学习与LLM生成个性化食谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是让用户借助手机相机识别其现有厨房食材，并据此自动生成可实际操作的食谱，输出为实时定制的烹饪指导，目标是简化找食谱流程、提升日常烹饪效率。

Motivation: 现有食物推荐系统存在明显缺口：寻找与现有食材匹配的食谱过程繁琐低效，且当前系统难以基于用户手头真实食材提供即时、实用的食谱，因此需要一套能兼顾识别准确性与端到端食谱生成的方案。

Method: 采用深度学习与LLM的双重方法：先用深度卷积网络完成食材识别，再将识别结果交由LLM生成详细且可实践的食谱；同时通过大规模数据增强与模型训练提升系统鲁棒性，并针对移动端算力约束选择合适骨干网络。

实验效果: 实验结果表明，用于食物识别的EfficientNet在准确率与计算效率之间取得平衡，适合移动端部署；摘要未报告具体精度数值或其他量化对比。

Abstract: Finding recipes that match available kitchen ingredients can be cumbersome and inefficient. This paper addresses this challenge by developing a mobile application that generates tailored food recipes based on ingredients identified using a mobile camera. Leveraging deep learning and large language model (LLM), our system offers a seamless and efficient solution for real-time recipe generation. By integrating deep learning for accurate ingredient recognition with LLM for generating detailed and practical recipes, our approach ensures users receive precise cooking instructions based on actual ingredients on hand. Extensive dataset augmentation and model training have resulted in a robust system capable of high performance. Experimental results demonstrate that EfficientNet, used for food recognition, offers a balance of accuracy and computational efficiency, making it ideal for mobile applications. This dual-approach method enhances the cooking experience, reduces food waste, and provides a comprehensive, real-time tool for everyday cooking needs, bridging a significant gap in current food recommendation systems.

</details>


### [244] [Multisensory Hybrid TV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33313)
*Fernando Boronat,Lluc Simó,Ángel González,Pau Noguera*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证了一种基于MPEG-V与HbbTV的端到端多感官混合（广播/宽带）电视方案，可同步传输视频流与感官效果元数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 任务是构建端到端多感官（mulsemedia）混合电视内容消费系统，输入为视频流与感官效果元数据（环境光、振动、气味、风、温度等），输出为符合HbbTV标准的可同步消费的多感官电视服务原型，目标是提升沉浸感与体验质量。

Motivation: 传统电视内容仅限视听流，缺少对其他感官的刺激；而多感官体验如何在混合广播/宽带环境下标准化、端到端地交付并保持同步，尚缺乏具体可行方案与实证评估。

Method: 采用MPEG-V表示感官效果元数据、基于HbbTV构建混合广播/宽带传输架构，并在符合HbbTV标准的端到端平台上实现原型，涵盖视频流与感官元数据的下发及同步消费，随后开展主观评测。

实验效果: 106名多样化背景的参与者主观评测结果高度满意，在整体愉悦度、真实感、沉浸感、感知同步性与QoE提升方面均表现良好；同时发现人口统计规律：学历越高者满意度普遍偏低、性别在感官接受度上存在偏好差异，且更高的真实感与沉浸感并未转化为相应的付费意愿，构成商业落地潜在障碍。

Abstract: Traditional TV media content exclusively involves audiovisual streams. Multi-sensorial media (mulsemedia) introduces sensory effects such as ambient lighting, vibration, scents, wind, or temperature to stimulate other senses and create more enjoyable, realistic, and immersive content consumption experiences. This paper presents an MPEG-V- and HbbTV-based end-to-end solution for providing multi-sensorial hybrid (broadcast/broadband) TV content consumption experiences. A prototype following the proposal has been implemented on an end-to-end HbbTV-compliant platform, including both video streams and sensory effects metadata delivery and synchronous consumption, replicating a mulsemedia hybrid TV service. The prototype has been subjectively evaluated and validated by 106 participants with diverse profiles, yielding highly satisfactory results, including overall enjoyment, realism, immersion, perceived synchronization, and QoE enhancement. Furthermore, the results have also revealed interesting demographic patterns, such as participants with higher academic levels reporting lower satisfaction scores in general, distinct gender-based preferences regarding sensory acceptance, and potential barriers to commercial adoption, where higher realism and immersion do not translate into an equivalent willingness to pay for these enhanced TV services.

</details>


### [245] [Can a Short Video Clip Verify a Model Fix?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33393)
*Raj Shan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 论文检验"短片段复现错误后重放该片段即可确认修复"这一常见做法是否可靠，发现在放大模型参数后其判定与原始输入上的参考结果常不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 在CG-Bench的960个预设问题上，将模型由Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct更新为Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct，考察开发者以"短片段错误复现—更新后重放"作为回归验证手段的有效性，目标是判断片段级通过能否代表原测试通过。

Motivation: 实践中开发者常用复现错误的短片段作为轻量回归测试替代原始长输入，但片段级通过与原测试通过之间的对应关系缺乏量化证据，若误用可能导致误报修复或漏报修复。

Method: 研究者预先冻结388个旧模型能精确复现其参考错误的片段，并在评分新模型前固定该集合；随后统计更新后片段通过/失败与原始参考结果的一致情况，得出片段通过对应的原测试通过率及遗漏率；另以1,028个视频、不同检查点与直接选项打分的独立研究做交叉验证。

实验效果: 64帧采样准确率由27.9%升至33.3%；片段通过中原参考通过者为51/116（44.0%，95% Wilson区间35.3–53.0%），片段失败中原参考失败者为16/272（5.9%）；仅凭片段通过会误判65个未解决失败为已修复，而要求片段通过则会漏掉67个真实修复中的16个（23.9%）；独立研究的1,028个视频中仅15个片段通过，16帧参考仅确认其中1个。

Abstract: A developer who reproduces a video model's error on a short clip can replay that clip after an update. How often does a correct clip answer mean the original test now passes? On 960 prespecified CG-Bench questions, we updated from Qwen2-VL-2B-Instruct to Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct. Accuracy on a 64-frame sample spanning the full video rose from 27.9% to 33.3%. Before scoring the newer model, we froze 388 clips on which the older model reproduced its exact reference error. The updated model passed the reference on 51 of 116 clip passes (44.0%, 95% Wilson interval 35.3-53.0%) and on 16 of 272 clip failures (5.9%). Closing cases on a clip pass would have declared 65 unresolved reference failures fixed. Requiring a clip pass would have missed 16 of the 67 reference fixes (23.9%). In a separate 1,028-video study with different checkpoints and direct option scoring, only 15 clips passed, and the 16-frame reference confirmed one. For the generated-answer update, the short replay identified cases worth checking, while the original input remained necessary to verify individual fixes.

</details>


### [246] [Hierarchical Semantic Learning for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis with Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34184)
*Chunlei Meng,Rong Fu,Zhan Chen,Yi Liu,Zhuo Zou,LiDa Xu,Chun Ouyang,Zhongxue Gan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 提出分层语义学习（HSL）框架，利用深度编码器的语义层次结构实现模态缺失下的鲁棒多模态情感分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
任务背景: 多模态情感分析在实际系统中需应对传感器故障、隐私屏蔽和传输不稳定导致的不可预测模态缺失，输入为多模态情感数据，输出为情感预测，目标是在多种缺失模式下保持鲁棒性能。

Motivation: 现有方法或学习可靠性感知表示、或重建缺失输入，但通常仅使用单一扁平化的编码器输出，忽视了深层编码器的语义层次以及模态内/模态间交互，导致语义平均化与病态映射问题。

Method: HSL包含四个组件：分层语义解耦从各模态编码器提取低、中、高三级特征流；跨模态跨层语义重建以观测模态的高层语义为全局锚点，阶梯式逐步重建缺失模态的中层结构与低层细节；分层级联误差监督采用原型对比正则约束观测与重建特征，强化层级语义一致性并抑制误差传播；分层交互融合捕捉跨模态、跨语义层的密集交互以生成鲁棒预测。

实验效果: 在CMU-MOSI、CMU-MOSEI、IEMOCAP和CH-SIMS四个基准上，HSL在大多数缺失模态设置下优于基线方法；摘要未报告具体数值指标。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis (MSA) in real-world systems must handle unpredictable modality missingness caused by sensor failures, privacy masking, and unstable transmission. Existing methods either learn reliability-aware representations or reconstruct missing inputs, often using a single flattened encoder output. This overlooks deep encoders' semantic hierarchy and intra- and inter-modal interactions, causing semantic averaging and ill-posed mappings. We propose Hierarchical Semantic Learning (HSL), a framework that exploits semantic hierarchy for robust MSA with missing modalities. Hierarchical Semantic Decoupling exposes low-, mid-, and high-level feature streams from each modality encoder. Cross-Modal Cross-Level Semantic Reconstruction performs ladder-style recovery, using high-level semantics from observed modalities as global anchors to progressively reconstruct mid-level structures and low-level details of missing modalities. To stabilize cross-level reconstruction, Hierarchical Cascade Error Supervisor applies prototypical contrastive regularization to observed and reconstructed features, enforcing level-specific semantic consistency and limiting error propagation. Finally, Hierarchical Interactive Fusion captures dense interactions across modalities and semantic levels to produce robust predictions under diverse missingness patterns. Experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, IEMOCAP, and CH-SIMS show that HSL outperforms baselines in most missing-modality settings.

</details>
